怀孕期间 RSVpreF 疫苗 的摄取:与社会经济地位、产前护理利用和孕产妇危险因素的关联。

Uptake of the RSVpreF vaccine during pregnancy: associations with socioeconomic status, prenatal care utilization, and maternal risk factors.
作者： Xiaofan Liu, Jonathan N. Cloughesy, Tianzhou Yu, Maddalena Ferranna
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿呼吸道疾病的主要原因,2023 年 8 月,基于 RSV 蛋白的 疫苗 (RSVpreF) 成为美国批准的第一个用于预防新生儿 RSV 感染的孕产妇 疫苗。
设计与对象
该研究估计了 RSVpreF 疫苗 在第一个 RSV 季节的吸收情况,并使用 Optum 的 Clinformatics® 数据集市数据库确定了与商业保险怀孕个体的吸收相关的因素。
核心方法
该研究纳入了2023年10月1日至2024年1月31日之间在妊娠32周或之后进行活产分娩的18-50岁个体,并从2022年7月1日起持续入组,直至分娩和可获得的社会经济记录。
主要结果
在 14,121 名孕妇中,14.5% 接受了 RSVpreF 疫苗;接种率从 2023 年 10 月 队列研究 的 1.5% 上升到 2024 年 1 月 队列研究 的 28.7%,并且 84.6% 在建议的 32-36 周妊娠窗口内接种了疫苗。
解释与创新
较高的吸收率与年龄较大、收入较高、受教育程度较高、PPO 保险、既往流感 疫苗接种、怀孕期间接受流感或 Tdap 疫苗、疫苗接种 窗口期增加几周以及对产前护理的更大依从性相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了社会人口因素、其他 疫苗 行为和产前护理依从性导致母亲 RSVpreF 疫苗 吸收的差异,表明了公平免疫计划改进的目标。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到商业保险人群和行政索赔数据的依赖(可能无法涵盖所有​​相关的临床或社会经济因素)以及批准后观察窗口较短的限制。

Uptake of the RSVpreF vaccine during pregnancy: associations with socioeconomic status, prenatal care utilization, and maternal risk factors.

Authors: Xiaofan Liu, Jonathan N. Cloughesy, Tianzhou Yu, Maddalena Ferranna
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of infant respiratory illness. In August 2023, the RSV protein-based vaccine (RSVpreF) became the first maternal vaccine approved in the United States to prevent RSV infection in newborns. The study aims to estimate RSVpreF vaccine uptake during its first RSV season and to identify key factors associated with uptake among commercially insured pregnant individuals, using Optum's Clinformatics® Data Mart Database. The study included individuals aged 18-50 with live birth deliveries at or after 32 weeks' gestation between October 1, 2023, and January 31, 2024, continuous enrollment from July 1, 2022, through delivery, and available socioeconomic records. Among 14,121 pregnant individuals,14.5% received the RSVpreF vaccine. Uptake rose from 1.5% among the October 2023 delivery cohort to 28.7% among the January 2024 cohort; 84.6% were vaccinated within the recommended 32-36 week gestational window. In multivariable models, higher uptake was associated with age (30-39 vs 18-29 years: OR = 1.52, 95% CI: 1.28-1.80), household income $60,000-$74,000 (OR = 1.40, 95% CI: 1.05-1.85) or $75,000-$99,000 (OR = 1.47, 95% CI: 1.13-1.92), bachelor's degree or higher (OR = 1.70, 95% CI: 1.48-1.96), PPO insurance (OR = 2.87, 95% CI: 1.70-4.87), prior influenza vaccination (OR = 1.78, 95% CI: 1.56-2.04), receipt of influenza (OR = 3.65, 95% CI: 3.14-4.24) or Tdap (OR = 4.40, 95% CI: 3.53-5.49) vaccines during pregnancy, and each additional week in the RSVpreF vaccination window(OR = 1.27; 95% CI: 1.06-1.53). Receiving ≥5 of 7 non-vaccine prenatal care services was also associated with higher uptake (OR = 1.82, 95% CI: 1.23-2.70). Comorbidities were not associated with uptake. Maternal RSVpreF vaccine uptake was low during its first on-market season, with significant disparities by sociodemographic factors, uptake of other vaccines, and adherence to recommended prenatal care. Efforts to improve equitable access and education are needed for effective maternal immunization programs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of infant respiratory illness, and in August 2023 the RSV protein-based vaccine (RSVpreF) became the first maternal vaccine approved in the United States to prevent RSV infection in newborns.
Design and population
The study estimated RSVpreF vaccine uptake during its first RSV season and identified factors associated with uptake among commercially insured pregnant individuals using Optum's Clinformatics® Data Mart Database.
Core methods
The study included individuals aged 18–50 with live birth deliveries at or after 32 weeks' gestation between October 1, 2023, and January 31, 2024, with continuous enrollment from July 1, 2022, through delivery and available socioeconomic records.
Main results
Among 14,121 pregnant individuals, 14.5% received the RSVpreF vaccine; uptake rose from 1.5% in the October 2023 delivery cohort to 28.7% in the January 2024 cohort, and 84.6% were vaccinated within the recommended 32–36 week gestational window.
Interpretation and novelty
Higher uptake was associated with older age, higher income, higher education, PPO insurance, prior influenza vaccination, receipt of influenza or Tdap vaccines during pregnancy, additional weeks in the vaccination window, and greater adherence to prenatal care.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight disparities in maternal RSVpreF vaccine uptake by sociodemographic factors, other vaccine behaviors, and prenatal care adherence, indicating targets for equitable immunization program improvement.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a commercially insured population and administrative claims data, which may not capture all relevant clinical or socioeconomic factors, and by the short post-approval observation window.

表征病原体-病原体相互作用的非机械方法的局限性:模拟研究。

The limitations of non-mechanistic methods for characterizing pathogen-pathogen interactions: A simulation study.
作者： Sarah C Kramer, Sarah Pirikahu, Cana Kussmaul, Lulla Opatowski, Matthieu Domenech de Cellès, Sarah Krämer
摘要中文翻译摘要:当一种病原体的感染影响另一种病原体感染或患病的机会时,就会发生病原体与病原体之间的相互作用。越来越多的证据表明,相互作用是传染病 流行病学 的一个共同特征。然而,由于传染病 传播 固有的非线性和随机性,以及混杂的频率(例如,通过共享季节性强迫),用于表征相互作用的简单、纯统计方法可能容易失败。在这里,我们进行了一项模拟研究,以评估从时间序列数据推断因果关系的几种更复杂的非机械方法:广义加性模型(GAM)、格兰杰因果关系、传递熵和收敛交叉映射(CCM)。具体来说,我们使用双病原体机制 传播 模型,经过校准以产生类似于流感 暴发 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的动力学,以生成具有一系列相互作用强度和持续时间值的合成数据集。然后,我们将每种方法应用于所有合成数据集。我们发现格兰杰因果关系、转移熵和 CCM 都无法一致地推断数据是否包含交互信号;特别是,方法往往会错误地识别未建模的相互作用(平均灵敏度分别为 80.6%、92.1%、72.1%;平均特异性 = 31.0%、33.3%、33.1%)。此外,我们发现每种方法的点估计与真实的相互作用强度之间几乎没有关联。相比之下,GAM 比其他方法更准确地推断相互作用的存在(灵敏度 = 85.2%,特异性 = 72.5%),并且对于更强的相互作用始终产生更大的点估计。然而,它们的实际用途受到无法评估相互作用不对称性的限制(即病原体 A 对病原体 B 的影响是否与 B 对 A 的影响相同)。当将方法应用于来自香港和加拿大的两个现实世界数据集时,总体性能模式相似。我们的结论是,基于 暴发 数据准确、全面地描述病原体与病原体之间的相互作用仍然是一个重大挑战。因此,在用于得出有关相互作用的结论之前,必须对任何提出的方法进行严格评估,这一点至关重要。作者总结 当一种病原体的感染增加或降低一个人因另一种不同的病原体而感染或患病的风险时,就会发生病原体与病原体之间的相互作用。由于相互作用会影响几种常见的人类病原体,包括流感和 SARS-CoV-2,因此更好地了解相互作用可以改善流行病控制。然而,过去的工作表明,通常用于研究相互作用的简单方法可能会导致不准确的结论。在这里,我们测试了生态学和神经科学等其他领域常用的四种方法,看看它们是否也可用于识别相互作用。具体来说,我们使用数学模型生成的模拟 暴发 数据测试了每种方法。我们发现大多数方法都难以正确确定是否存在交互效应;特别是,当没有发生相互作用时,方法经常错误地识别相互作用。尽管其中一种测试方法(广义加性模型)在识别相互作用方面表现相对较好,但它提供的有关相互作用的附加信息相对较少。由于病原体与病原体之间的相互作用的研究非常具有挑战性,因此研究人员在将方法应用于相互作用之前必须严格测试方法,以免发表潜在的误导性结果。更广泛地说,对相互作用的完整理解可能需要多种方法,包括实验室和建模研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
病原体与病原体之间的相互作用会影响一种病原体引起另一种病原体的感染或疾病的风险,并且这种相互作用越来越被认为在传染病 流行病学 中很常见。该研究强调了在这种情况下从时间序列数据推断因果关系的非机械统计方法的可靠性方面的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究使用由校准为类似于流感和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 动力学的双病原体机械 传播 模型生成的合成 暴发 数据集,对四种非机械方法(广义加性模型 (GAM)、格兰杰因果关系、转移熵和收敛交叉映射 (CCM))进行基于模拟的评估。
核心方法
使用机械模型创建具有不同相互作用强度和持续时间的综合数据集,并将每种方法应用于所有数据集,以评估它们推断相互作用存在和强度的能力。这些方法还在来自香港和加拿大的两个真实数据集上进行了测试。
主要结果
格兰杰因果关系、转移熵和 CCM 经常无法正确识别相互作用,平均灵敏度分别为 80.6%、92.1% 和 72.1%,特异度分别为 31.0%、33.3% 和 33.1%。GAM 表现更好,灵敏度为 85.2%,特异性为 72.5%,并且对于更强的相互作用显示出更大的点估计。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,从 暴发 数据中准确描述病原体与病原体之间的相互作用仍然具有挑战性,因为大多数方法都难以区分真实的相互作用和噪音。GAM 显示了相对较好的性能,但实用性仍然有限,特别是在评估交互不对称性方面。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果警告不要使用未经验证的非机械方法来推断病原体与病原体之间的相互作用,这可能会导致误导性结论,对 监测 和流行病控制策略产生影响。在将方法应用于实际数据之前,对方法进行严格的评估至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到对模拟数据的依赖以及 GAM 无法评估交互不对称性的限制。尽管模拟框架提供了受控比较,但由于仅限抽象范围且缺乏两个数据集之外的现实世界验证,证据强度中等。

The limitations of non-mechanistic methods for characterizing pathogen-pathogen interactions: A simulation study.

Authors: Sarah C Kramer, Sarah Pirikahu, Cana Kussmaul, Lulla Opatowski, Matthieu Domenech de Cellès, Sarah Krämer
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Pathogen-pathogen interactions occur when infection with one pathogen influences one’s chance of infection or disease due to another. Increasingly, evidence suggests that interactions are a common feature of infectious disease epidemiology. However, due to both the nonlinearities and stochasticity inherent to infectious disease transmission, and the frequency of confounding (e.g., by shared seasonal forcing), simple, purely statistical methods for characterizing interactions may be prone to failure. Here, we perform a simulation study to evaluate several more complex non-mechanistic approaches for inferring causality from time series data: generalized additive models (GAMs), Granger causality, transfer entropy, and convergent cross-mapping (CCM). Specifically, we use a two-pathogen mechanistic transmission model, calibrated to produce dynamics resembling outbreaks of influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), to generate synthetic datasets with a range of values for interaction strength and duration. We then apply each method to all synthetic datasets. We find that Granger causality, transfer entropy, and CCM all fail to consistently infer whether data contain signal of an interaction; in particular, methods tend to incorrectly identify interactions where none are modeled (average sensitivity = 80.6%, 92.1%, 72.1%, respectively; average specificity = 31.0%, 33.3%, 33.1%). Furthermore, we find little to no association between point estimates from each method and true interaction strength. In contrast, GAMs infer the existence of interactions more accurately than the other methods (sensitivity = 85.2%, specificity = 72.5%), and consistently yield larger point estimates for stronger interactions. However, their practical utility is limited by an inability to evaluate interaction asymmetry (i.e., whether the effect of pathogen A on pathogen B is identical to that of B on A). Overall performance patterns were similar when methods were applied to two real-world datasets from Hong Kong and Canada. We conclude that accurately and comprehensively characterizing pathogen-pathogen interactions based on outbreak data remains a significant challenge. For this reason, it is critical that any proposed methods be rigorously evaluated before being used to draw conclusions about interactions. Author Summary Pathogen-pathogen interactions occur when infection with one pathogen either increases or decreases a person’s risk of infection or illness due to a second, distinct pathogen. Because interactions affect several common human pathogens, including influenza and SARS-CoV-2, a better understanding of interactions could improve epidemic control. However, past work has shown that simple methods commonly used to study interactions can lead to inaccurate conclusions. Here, we tested four methods frequently used in other fields, including ecology and neuroscience, to see whether they may also be useful for identifying interactions. Specifically, we tested each method using simulated outbreak data generated from a mathematical model. We found that most methods struggled to correctly determine whether an interaction effect was present; in particular, methods often falsely identified interactions when none occurred. Although one of the tested methods, generalized additive models, performed comparatively well at identifying interactions, it provided relatively little additional information about the interactions. Because pathogen-pathogen interactions are so challenging to study, it is important that researchers rigorously test methods before applying them to interactions, so as not to publish potentially misleading results. More broadly, a complete understanding of interactions will likely require a variety of approaches, including both laboratory and modeling studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Pathogen-pathogen interactions influence the risk of infection or disease from one pathogen due to another, and such interactions are increasingly recognized as common in infectious disease epidemiology. The study highlights a knowledge gap regarding the reliability of non-mechanistic statistical methods for inferring causality from time-series data in this context.
Design and population
The study performed a simulation-based evaluation of four non-mechanistic methods—generalized additive models (GAMs), Granger causality, transfer entropy, and convergent cross-mapping (CCM)—using synthetic outbreak datasets generated from a two-pathogen mechanistic transmission model calibrated to resemble influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) dynamics.
Core methods
Synthetic datasets were created with varying interaction strengths and durations using a mechanistic model, and each method was applied to all datasets to assess their ability to infer interaction presence and strength. The methods were also tested on two real-world datasets from Hong Kong and Canada.
Main results
Granger causality, transfer entropy, and CCM frequently failed to correctly identify interactions, with average sensitivities of 80.6%, 92.1%, and 72.1% and specificities of 31.0%, 33.3%, and 33.1%, respectively. GAMs performed better, with sensitivity of 85.2% and specificity of 72.5%, and showed larger point estimates for stronger interactions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that accurately characterizing pathogen-pathogen interactions from outbreak data remains challenging, as most methods struggle to distinguish true interactions from noise. GAMs showed comparatively better performance but still had limited utility, particularly in assessing interaction asymmetry.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings caution against using unvalidated non-mechanistic methods to infer pathogen-pathogen interactions, which could lead to misleading conclusions with implications for surveillance and epidemic control strategies. Rigorous evaluation of methods is essential before applying them to real-world data.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the reliance on simulated data and the inability of GAMs to evaluate interaction asymmetry. The evidence strength is moderate due to the abstract-only scope and the lack of real-world validation beyond two datasets, though the simulation framework provides controlled comparisons.

评估母体 RSV 疫苗 (RSVpreF) 针对婴儿 毛细支气管炎 的摄取、安全性和有效性:初步现实世界证据

Evaluating the Uptake, Safety and Effectiveness of the Maternal RSV Vaccine (RSVpreF) Against Infant Bronchiolitis: Preliminary Real-World Evidence
作者： Genevieve Haddock, Melissa Ng, Thomas Johnson, Anthony Vakis, Simon B. Drysdale
摘要中文翻译背景:呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是全球婴儿住院的主要原因。2024 年 9 月 1 日,英国在获得安全性和有效性试验证据后推出了二价 RSVpreF 疫苗 用于孕产妇免疫。然而,有关吸收、安全性和有效性的现实数据仍然有限。方法:我们使用从牛津大学医院 NHS 基金会信托基金定期收集的电子健康记录数据进行了回顾性审计。2024年9月1日至10月31日期间的所有活产婴儿均包含在内。分析了母亲 RSV 疫苗接种 状态、人口因素和围产期结局。跟踪了 2024 年 11 月 1 日至 2025 年 3 月 12 日期间与 RSV 相关的住院情况。疫苗 有效性 (VE) 计算为 1 -(接种疫苗的风险/未接种疫苗的风险)× 100。结果:在 1057 名活产婴儿中,341 名母亲(32.3%)在怀孕期间接受了 RSVpreF 疫苗接种。高龄母亲和初产妇的摄取量较高,并且与接受百日咳 疫苗 密切相关(41.3% 对比 11.6%;P 为 疫苗接种 后 14 天以及 772 名未接种疫苗的婴儿中的 36 名),在 6 个月以下的婴儿中,粗 VE 为 78.3%(95% 置信区间,9.1-94.8)。VE 相对于 毛细支气管炎 编码的入院率为 56.4%(95% 置信区间,13.1-78.2)。结论:在英国实施的第一个季节,母亲 RSV 疫苗接种 与婴儿 RSV 住院治疗相关,但早期安全性数据仍然较低,需要继续进行。支持国家计划监测和整合。

解释与创新
在英国第一个实施季节期间,母亲 RSV 疫苗接种 与婴儿 RSV 住院的实质性保护有关。

Evaluating the Uptake, Safety and Effectiveness of the Maternal RSV Vaccine (RSVpreF) Against Infant Bronchiolitis: Preliminary Real-World Evidence

Authors: Genevieve Haddock, Melissa Ng, Thomas Johnson, Anthony Vakis, Simon B. Drysdale
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of infant hospitalization globally. On September 1, 2024, England introduced the bivalent RSVpreF vaccine for maternal immunization following trial evidence of safety and efficacy. However, real-world data on uptake, safety and effectiveness remain limited. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective audit using routinely collected electronic health record data from Oxford University Hospitals NHS Foundation Trust. All live births between September 1 and October 31, 2024, were included. Maternal RSV vaccination status, demographic factors and perinatal outcomes were analyzed. RSV-related hospitalizations were tracked from November 1, 2024, to March 12, 2025. Vaccine effectiveness (VE) was calculated as 1 - (risk in vaccinated/risk in unvaccinated) × 100. RESULTS: Among 1057 live births, 341 mothers (32.3%) received RSVpreF vaccination during pregnancy. Uptake was higher in older and primiparous mothers and strongly associated with receipt of pertussis vaccine (41.3% vs. 11.6%; P 14 days post-vaccination and in 36 of 772 unvaccinated infants, yielding a crude VE of 78.3% (95% confidence interval, 9.1-94.8) in infants <6 months of age. VE against bronchiolitis-coded admissions was 56.4% (95% confidence interval, 13.1-78.2). CONCLUSIONS: Maternal RSV vaccination was associated with substantial protection against infant RSV hospitalization during the first UK season of implementation. Uptake improved over time but remained relatively low. Early safety data are reassuring. Continued surveillance and larger multisite evaluations are needed to support national program monitoring and integration.

2019年冠状病毒病大流行之前、期间和之后住院儿童肺炎支原体肺炎的趋势和结果:2018-2023年中国东北地区的回顾性研究。

Trends and outcomes of Mycoplasma pneumoniae pneumonia in hospitalized children before, during, and after the coronavirus disease 2019 pandemic: a retrospective study in Northeast China, 2018-2023.
作者： Chunxue Jiang, Xin Chen, Xuewen Xu, Tingting Sun, Wei Xu, Kai You
摘要中文翻译摘要 肺炎支原体肺炎(MPP)是最常见的社区获得性肺炎,流行间隔为3-7年。本研究旨在了解 2019 年冠状病毒病大流行之前和期间住院儿童 MPP 的变化趋势,并确定不良结果及其影响因素。这是一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,对象为 2018 年 1 月至 2023 年 12 月期间在我们机构因 MPP 住院的 28 天至 14 岁儿童。使用卡方检验和多变量逻辑回归分析临床特征、不良结果和相关因素。P < 0.05 被认为具有统计显着性。共纳入17,505例MPP病例,其中2023年病例数最高(4,619例)。2023年重症肺炎比例为44.1%,支气管镜使用率为33.5%,均显着高于2018-2022年。年龄较大、7月至12月入院、合并EB病毒、衣原体或其他细菌感染以及并发症,包括胸腔积液、肝酶异常、急性呼吸窘迫综合征或肺不张,均与支气管镜检查有关。年龄较大、10月至12月期间入院、合并感染呼吸道合胞病毒、腺病毒、EB病毒、衣原体或其他细菌,以及并发症,包括心力衰竭、肝酶异常、心肌损伤或贫血,均与重症肺炎相关。与前五年相比,2023 年 MPP、重症肺炎和需要支气管镜检查的病例有所增加。MPP 儿童更容易出现不良临床结果,这与年龄、入院月份、合并感染和并发症有关。重要性 肺炎支原体肺炎 (MPP) 是儿童社区获得性肺炎的主要原因。本研究分析了 2018 年至 2023 年中国东北地区的 17,505 名住院儿童,探讨 2019 年冠状病毒病大流行之前、期间和之后的 MPP 趋势。我们发现 2023 年 MPP 急剧复苏,严重肺炎、并发症和支气管镜检查的使用率更高。不良结果的关键危险因素包括年龄较大、10月至12月入院、病毒/细菌混合感染以及肺部或肺外并发症。这些发现提高了对大流行后 MPP 流行病学 的理解,支持早期风险分层,并有助于优化儿童 MPP 的临床管理和公共卫生干预措施。

Trends and outcomes of Mycoplasma pneumoniae pneumonia in hospitalized children before, during, and after the coronavirus disease 2019 pandemic: a retrospective study in Northeast China, 2018-2023.

Authors: Chunxue Jiang, Xin Chen, Xuewen Xu, Tingting Sun, Wei Xu, Kai You
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Mycoplasma pneumoniae pneumonia (MPP) is the most common form of community-acquired pneumonia, which shows an epidemic interval of 3–7 years. This study was conducted to understand the changing trends in MPP in children hospitalized before and during the coronavirus disease 2019 pandemic and determine adverse outcomes and their influencing factors. This was a retrospective cohort study of children aged 28 days to 14 years hospitalized for MPP at our institution between January 2018 and December 2023. Clinical characteristics, adverse outcomes, and associated factors were analyzed using the chi-square test and multivariable logistic regression. A P < 0.05 was considered statistically significant. A total of 17,505 MPP cases were included, with the highest case number (4,619) in 2023. In 2023, the proportion of severe pneumonia was 44.1%, and bronchoscopy usage was 33.5%, both significantly higher than in 2018–2022. Older age, admission during July–December, co-infection with Epstein-Barr virus, chlamydia, or other bacteria, and complications, including pleural effusion, abnormal liver enzymes, acute respiratory distress syndrome, or atelectasis, were associated with bronchoscopy. Older age, admission during October–December, co-infection with respiratory syncytial virus, adenovirus, Epstein-Barr virus, chlamydia, or other bacteria, and complications, including cardiac failure, abnormal liver enzymes, myocardial damage, or anemia, were associated with severe pneumonia. Cases of MPP, severe pneumonia, and those requiring bronchoscopy increased in 2023 compared with the five previous years. Children with MPP were more susceptible to adverse clinical outcomes, which were associated with age, month of admission, co-infection, and complications. IMPORTANCE Mycoplasma pneumoniae pneumonia (MPP) is a leading cause of community-acquired pneumonia in children. This study analyzed 17,505 hospitalized children in Northeast China from 2018 to 2023 to explore MPP trends before, during, and after the coronavirus disease 2019 pandemic. We found a sharp resurgence of MPP in 2023 with higher rates of severe pneumonia, complications, and bronchoscopy use. Key risk factors for adverse outcomes included older age, admission in October–December, viral/bacterial co-infection, and pulmonary or extrapulmonary complications. These findings improve understanding of post-pandemic MPP epidemiology, support early risk stratification, and help optimize clinical management and public health interventions for childhood MPP.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to understand the changing trends in Mycoplasma pneumoniae pneumonia (MPP) in children hospitalized before and during the coronavirus disease 2019 pandemic and determine adverse outcomes and their influencing factors.
Design and population
The study was a retrospective cohort study of children aged 28 days to 14 years hospitalized for MPP at the institution between January 2018 and December 2023. It analyzed 17,505 hospitalized children in Northeast China from 2018 to 2023 to explore MPP trends before, during, and after the coronavirus disease 2019 pandemic.
Core methods
Clinical characteristics, adverse outcomes, and associated factors were analyzed using the chi-square test and multivariable logistic regression.
Main results
Cases of MPP, severe pneumonia, and those requiring bronchoscopy increased in 2023 compared with the five previous years. In 2023, the proportion of severe pneumonia was 44.1%, and bronchoscopy usage was 33.5%, both significantly higher than in 2018–2022. Older age, admission during July–December, co-infection with Epstein-Barr virus, chlamydia, or other bacteria, and complications, including pleural effusion, abnormal liver enzymes, acute respiratory distress syndrome, or atelectasis, were associated with bronchoscopy.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating a sharp resurgence of MPP in 2023 with higher rates of severe pneumonia, complications, and bronchoscopy use, which highlights the need for early risk stratification and optimized clinical management.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings improve understanding of post-pandemic MPP epidemiology, support early risk stratification, and help optimize clinical management and public health interventions for childhood MPP.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential biases in data collection, but the evidence is strong due to the large sample size and statistical analyses.

低脐带血滴度与早产儿母体 RSV 抗体半衰期延长有关

Low Cord Blood Titers Are Associated with Prolonged Maternal RSV Antibody Half-Lives in Preterm Infants
作者： Joukje E. Willemsen, Emily W E M Phijffer, Joanne G Wildenbeest, Larry J. Anderson, Binh Ha, Ranjini Sankaranarayanan, Floris Groenendaal, Kitty W M Bloemenkamp, José A M Borghans, Julia Drylewicz
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 早产儿发生严重 RSV 感染的风险较高,部分原因是母体抗体 (Abs) 胎盘转移减少。在生命的第一年,严重感染的风险持续增加。本研究评估了未接种疫苗的母亲所生早产儿和足月儿 RSV 中和抗体的产后动力学。方法 我们招募了未接种 RSV 疫苗的母亲在医院出生的胎龄 (GA) 为 24-42 周的婴儿。在出生时(脐带血)、1-2 周和 4-8 周收集血清。使用线性混合效应模型来估计与 GA 和脐带血滴度相关的 RSV 中和抗体的半衰期。对总血清 IgG1 进行了重复分析,以证实我们研究结果的稳健性。结果 169 名婴儿中,95 名早产(<37 周 GA,其中 53 名新生儿<32 周 GA)。早产与 RSV 中和抗体半衰期延长有关(极早产儿约 79 天,足月儿约 30 天)。胎龄与抗体半衰期之间的负相关很大程度上是由脐带血抗体水平与抗体半衰期之间的负相关引起的。脐带血滴度加倍导致 RSV 中和抗体半衰期缩短约 20%,IgG1 半衰期缩短约 32%。结论 出生后抗体半衰期取决于起始水平:滴度越高,下降速度越快。如果我们的观察结果对 疫苗 诱导的抗体进行重复,那么早产儿和足月儿之间 疫苗 保护的差异可能比预期的要小。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于母体抗体胎盘转移减少,早产儿发生严重 RSV 感染的风险较高。本研究旨在评估未接种疫苗的母亲所生早产儿和足月儿 RSV 中和抗体的产后动力学。
设计与对象
该研究纳入了未接种 RSV 疫苗的母亲在医院出生的胎龄 (GA) 为 24-42 周的婴儿。在 169 名婴儿中,95 名早产(<37 周 GA,其中 53 名新生儿<32 周 GA)。
核心方法
在出生时(脐带血)、1-2 周和 4-8 周收集血清。使用线性混合效应模型来估计与 GA 和脐带血滴度相关的 RSV 中和抗体的半衰期。对总血清 IgG1 进行了重复分析,以证实我们研究结果的稳健性。
主要结果
早产与 RSV 中和抗体半衰期延长有关(极早产儿约 79 天,足月儿约 30 天)。胎龄与抗体半衰期之间的负相关很大程度上是由脐带血抗体水平与抗体半衰期之间的负相关引起的。脐带血滴度加倍导致 RSV 中和抗体半衰期缩短约 20%,IgG1 半衰期缩短约 32%。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,出生后抗体半衰期取决于起始水平:滴度越高,下降速度越快。这一发现表明,如果对 疫苗 诱导的抗体重复我们的观察结果,早产儿和足月儿之间 疫苗 保护的差异可能小于预期。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为针对早产儿制定更有效的 疫苗接种 策略提供信息,因为它们强调了初始抗体水平与其产后下降之间的关系,这可能会影响免疫接种的时机和效果。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于未接种 RSV 的母亲所生的婴儿,结果可能无法推广到所有人群。证据是强有力的,因为它基于线性混合效应模型和 IgG1 的重复分析。

Low Cord Blood Titers Are Associated with Prolonged Maternal RSV Antibody Half-Lives in Preterm Infants

Authors: Joukje E. Willemsen, Emily W E M Phijffer, Joanne G Wildenbeest, Larry J. Anderson, Binh Ha, Ranjini Sankaranarayanan, Floris Groenendaal, Kitty W M Bloemenkamp, José A M Borghans, Julia Drylewicz
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Preterm infants are at higher risk of severe RSV infection, partially because of reduced placental transfer of maternal antibodies (Abs). Increased risk of severe infection persists in the first year of life. This study evaluates the postnatal kinetics of RSV neutralizing antibodies in preterm and full-term infants born to unvaccinated mothers. Methods We enrolled infants with a gestational age (GA) of 24-42 weeks born in hospital from RSV-unvaccinated mothers. Serum was collected at birth (cord blood), 1–2 weeks, and 4–8 weeks. Linear mixed-effects models were used to estimate half-lives of RSV-neutralizing Abs in relation to GA and cord blood titer. Analyses were repeated for total serum IgG1 to confirm the robustness of our findings. Results Of 169 infants, 95 were born preterm (<37 weeks GA, including 53 neonates <32 weeks GA). Premature birth was related to prolonged half-life of RSV-neutralizing Abs (∼79 days very preterm to ∼30 days in term infants). The inverse association between gestational age and antibody half-life was largely accounted for by an inverse association between cord blood Ab levels and Ab half-life. A doubling in cord blood titers led to a reduction in half-life of ∼20% for RSV neutralizing Abs and ∼32% for IgG1. Conclusions Postnatal antibody half-life depends on starting levels: higher titers decline faster. If our observation would be replicated for vaccine-induced Abs, the difference in vaccine protection between preterm and term infants may be smaller than anticipated.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Preterm infants are at higher risk of severe RSV infection due to reduced placental transfer of maternal antibodies. This study aimed to evaluate the postnatal kinetics of RSV neutralizing antibodies in preterm and full-term infants born to unvaccinated mothers.
Design and population
The study enrolled infants with a gestational age (GA) of 24-42 weeks born in hospital from RSV-unvaccinated mothers. Of 169 infants, 95 were born preterm (<37 weeks GA, including 53 neonates <32 weeks GA).
Core methods
Serum was collected at birth (cord blood), 1–2 weeks, and 4–8 weeks. Linear mixed-effects models were used to estimate half-lives of RSV-neutralizing Abs in relation to GA and cord blood titer. Analyses were repeated for total serum IgG1 to confirm the robustness of our findings.
Main results
Premature birth was related to prolonged half-life of RSV-neutralizing Abs (∼79 days very preterm to ∼30 days in term infants). The inverse association between gestational age and antibody half-life was largely accounted for by an inverse association between cord blood Ab levels and Ab half-life. A doubling in cord blood titers led to a reduction in half-life of ∼20% for RSV neutralizing Abs and ∼32% for IgG1.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that postnatal antibody half-life depends on starting levels: higher titers decline faster. This finding suggests that the difference in vaccine protection between preterm and term infants may be smaller than anticipated if our observation is replicated for vaccine-induced Abs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of more effective vaccination strategies for preterm infants, as they highlight the relationship between initial antibody levels and their postnatal decline, which could impact the timing and efficacy of immunization.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to infants born to RSV-unvaccinated mothers, and the results may not be generalizable to all populations. The evidence is robust as it is based on linear mixed-effects models and repeated analyses for IgG1.

毛细支气管炎 误诊和抗生素滥用——来自孟加拉国最大儿科医院的经验。

Misdiagnosis of bronchiolitis and misuse of antibiotics - experience from the largest paediatric hospital of Bangladesh.
作者： Farhat Lamisa Kabir, Luthful Kabir Arm, Jahangir Alam, Kazi Selim Anwar, Md Ruhul Amin, Probir Kumar Sarker, Samir Kumar Saha, Rahat Bin Habib, Abid Hossain Mollah, Mohammod Jobayer Chisti
摘要中文翻译背景 毛细支气管炎 仍然是全球幼儿中最常见的急性下呼吸道感染。由于密切相关的临床表现,毛细支气管炎 经常被误诊为世界卫生组织 (WHO) 分类的肺炎。我们的主要目标是评估 WHO 分类肺炎中病毒 毛细支气管炎 的 患病率,次要目标是评估其管理。方法这是一项前瞻性观察研究。婴儿(对 60 例 WHO 分类的肺炎病例进行 NPA 的年龄结果 PCR,其中 41 例(68%)具有提示 毛细支气管炎 的相关临床症状和体征,经良性胸片证实(85%)。此外,他们的平均值 (x̄) 和标准差 (SD) 全血细胞计数 (x̄ = 15,488;SD = 1874/cm),其中 x̄ = 36% (SD = 11) 中性粒细胞) 和 C 反应蛋白 (x̄ = 4.49 (5.88) mg/L 截止参考值 >5 mg/L) 更能表明非细菌感染有利于 RSV 毛细支气管炎。相比之下,所有 19 例非 RSV 病例在临床表现上没有差异,经血液学、微生物学和放射学特征证明,表明 毛细支气管炎 样疾病,并得到疾病结果、出院模式和 LOS(x̄ = 4.7;SD = 2.29)天的进一步支持。尽管如此,所有 60 例病例中 95% 都使用了抗生素。结论 WHO分类肺炎中毛细支气管炎比例较高,需给予抗菌药物治疗。研究结果需要政策制定者的关注,以解决世界卫生组织分类的肺炎和 毛细支气管炎 之间的重叠问题,并最大限度地减少潜在的抗生素过度使用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于临床特征重叠,毛细支气管炎 经常被误诊为 WHO 分类的肺炎,导致不必要的抗生素使用。该研究旨在量化肺炎婴儿中 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 相关 毛细支气管炎 的 患病率 并评估其临床管理。
设计与对象
这是在孟加拉国一家儿科医院进行的一项前瞻性观察研究。其中包括 60 名 2 岁以下婴儿,他们被归类为世界卫生组织定义的肺炎。收集鼻咽抽吸物用于 RSV 检测。
核心方法
该研究使用鼻咽抽吸物的 PCR 检测来检测 RSV。收集临床管理数据,包括抗生素处方实践。评估血液学参数和胸片以区分 毛细支气管炎 和肺炎。
主要结果
PCR 检测在 60 例病例中发现了 41 例 (68%) 存在 RSV,所有病例的临床特征均符合 毛细支气管炎 和良性胸部 X 光片 (85%)。非 RSV 病例 (19/60) 显示出相似的临床和影像学特征,提示 毛细支气管炎 样疾病。尽管 RSV 呈阳性,但在所有 60 例病例中,95% 的患者都服用了抗生素。
解释与创新
WHO 分类的肺炎病例中 RSV 相关 毛细支气管炎 的比例很高,凸显了诊断挑战,并导致抗生素过度使用。该研究强调需要改进 毛细支气管炎 和肺炎之间的区分,以指导适当的 治疗。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持针对有肺炎样症状的婴儿进行 RSV 针对性 监测 治疗,以减少不必要的抗生素处方。这可以为资源有限环境中呼吸道感染管理的国家指南提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其单中心设计和小样本量而受到限制,这可能会影响普遍性。缺乏对照组和对观察数据的依赖降低了关于抗生素过度使用的因果推论的强度。

Misdiagnosis of bronchiolitis and misuse of antibiotics - experience from the largest paediatric hospital of Bangladesh.

Authors: Farhat Lamisa Kabir, Luthful Kabir Arm, Jahangir Alam, Kazi Selim Anwar, Md Ruhul Amin, Probir Kumar Sarker, Samir Kumar Saha, Rahat Bin Habib, Abid Hossain Mollah, Mohammod Jobayer Chisti
Original abstract (English)
Background Bronchiolitis remains the commonest acute lower respiratory tract infection in younger children globally. Bronchiolitis is often misdiagnosed as World Health Organization (WHO)-classified pneumonia due to closely related clinical manifestations. Our primary objective was to assess the prevalence of viral bronchiolitis among WHO-classified pneumonia, and the secondary objective was to evaluate their management. Methods This was a prospective observational study. Infants (aged Results PCR of NPA of 60 WHO-classified pneumonia cases yielded RSV in 41 (68%) who had pertinent clinical symptoms and signs suggestive of bronchiolitis, attested by benign chest radiography (85%). Further, their mean (x̄) and standard deviation (SD) complete blood count (x̄ = 15,488; SD = 1874/cm) with x̄ = 36% (SD = 11) neutrophil) and C-reactive protein (x̄ = 4.49 (5.88) mg/L cut off reference value >5 mg/L) were indicative more as non-bacterial infections favouring RSV bronchiolitis. In contrast, all 19 non-RSV cases did not differ in clinical findings, as attested by haematological, microbiological and radiographical features, suggesting bronchiolitis-like illness, further supported by disease outcome, discharge pattern and LOS (x̄ = 4.7; SD = 2.29) days). Nevertheless, antibiotics were given in 95% of all 60 cases. Conclusions There was a high proportion of bronchiolitis among WHO-classified pneumonia, and antimicrobials were prescribed to treat those cases. The findings need the attention of policymakers to address the overlap between WHO-classified pneumonia and bronchiolitis and minimise the potential overuse of antibiotics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Bronchiolitis is frequently misdiagnosed as WHO-classified pneumonia due to overlapping clinical features, leading to unnecessary antibiotic use. The study aimed to quantify the prevalence of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV)-associated bronchiolitis among infants classified with pneumonia and to evaluate their clinical management.
Design and population
This was a prospective observational study conducted in a paediatric hospital in Bangladesh. It included 60 infants aged under 2 years who were classified with WHO-defined pneumonia. Nasopharyngeal aspirates were collected for RSV testing.
Core methods
The study used PCR testing of nasopharyngeal aspirates to detect RSV. Clinical management data were collected, including antibiotic prescription practices. Haematological parameters and chest radiography were assessed to differentiate bronchiolitis from pneumonia.
Main results
PCR testing identified RSV in 41 of 60 cases (68%), all of whom had clinical features consistent with bronchiolitis and benign chest radiographs (85%). Non-RSV cases (19/60) showed similar clinical and radiographic features, suggesting bronchiolitis-like illness. Antibiotics were prescribed in 95% of all 60 cases despite RSV positivity.
Interpretation and novelty
The high proportion of RSV-associated bronchiolitis among WHO-classified pneumonia cases highlights diagnostic challenges and contributes to antibiotic overuse. The study underscores the need for improved differentiation between bronchiolitis and pneumonia to guide appropriate treatment.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support targeted surveillance for RSV in infants with pneumonia-like symptoms to reduce unnecessary antibiotic prescriptions. This could inform national guidelines on respiratory infection management in resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single-centre design and small sample size, which may affect generalisability. The lack of a control group and reliance on observational data reduce the strength of causal inferences about antibiotic overuse.

疫苗 可预防的呼吸道疾病在北欧国家 65 岁及以上成年人中的流行病学和经济负担

The Epidemiological and Economic Burden of Vaccine-Preventable Respiratory Diseases Among Adults Aged 65 Years and Older in the Nordic Countries
作者： Nicoline Weinreich Reinstrup, Renée Hangaard Gidsel, Terho Heikkinen, Anne Ma Dyrhol‐Riise, Lars-Åke Levin, Lars Østergaard, Lars Holger Ehlers, A. Dyrhol-Riise
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了 疫苗 可预防呼吸道疾病 (VPRD) 对老年人造成的持续公共卫生和经济负担,尽管有 疫苗,明确将 疫苗 (RSV) 纳入目标。
设计与对象
静态模型量化了 2024/2025 年冬季北欧四个国家(丹麦、瑞典、挪威和芬兰)65 岁及以上成年人中 VPRD 的流行病学和经济负担。
核心方法
将2024年21月21日至2025年20日的发病率、住院人数和发病率的全国统计数据与同行评审的文献相结合,建立估计负担的静态模型。
主要结果
2024/2025 年季节,RSV 导致北欧国家约 105,000 例有症状病例、7,500 例住院病例和 970 例死亡。
解释与创新
作者将 RSV 与其他 VPRD 的持续高负担解释为当前免疫工作仍然不足的证据,强调 RSV 是扩大 疫苗 策略的优先事项。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持通过提高 疫苗 的吸收率、扩大覆盖范围以及采用先进的 RSV 疫苗 来减少住院和死亡,从而加强成人免疫计划。
局限与证据强度
静态模型依赖于国家统计数据和文献,由于报告不足和 RSV 特定数据有限,可能会低估真实负担,因此需要进行适度的置信度评估。

The Epidemiological and Economic Burden of Vaccine-Preventable Respiratory Diseases Among Adults Aged 65 Years and Older in the Nordic Countries

Authors: Nicoline Weinreich Reinstrup, Renée Hangaard Gidsel, Terho Heikkinen, Anne Ma Dyrhol‐Riise, Lars-Åke Levin, Lars Østergaard, Lars Holger Ehlers, A. Dyrhol-Riise
Original abstract (English)
Background: Despite the increasing availability of tailored vaccines, vaccine-preventable respiratory diseases (VPRDs), including influenza, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), COVID-19, and pneumococcal disease (PD), continue to impose a significant health and economic burden. This study assessed the epidemiological and economic burden of VPRD among adults aged ≥65 across four Nordic countries (Denmark, Sweden, Norway, and Finland). Methods: Data on incidence, hospital admissions, and mortality were obtained from national statistics (week 21, 2024–week 20, 2025) and supplemented with peer-reviewed literature. A static model was developed to quantify epidemiological and economic burden associated with VPRD during the 2024/2025 winter season. Results: During the 2024/2025 season, VPRD resulted in approximately 830,000 symptomatic cases, 60,300 hospital admissions, and 7750 deaths across the Nordic countries. COVID-19 showed the highest estimated incidence and numbers of hospital admissions, followed by influenza, PD, and RSV. Mortality was comparable between COVID-19 and influenza, followed by PD and RSV. Annual healthcare costs were approximately €1132 million, with productivity losses constituting an additional 7%. Conclusions: Despite national vaccination efforts, VPRDs remain a major health and economic burden in the Nordic countries. Strengthening immunization programs through improved vaccine uptake, broader coverage, and use of advanced vaccines may contribute to further reducing this burden.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the ongoing public-health and economic burden imposed by vaccine-preventable respiratory diseases (VPRDs) in older adults despite available vaccines, explicitly including respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) among the targets.
Design and population
A static model quantified the epidemiological and economic burden of VPRDs during the 2024/2025 winter season across four Nordic countries (Denmark, Sweden, Norway, and Finland) among adults aged 65 years and older.
Core methods
National statistics on incidence, hospital admissions, and mortality from week 21, 2024 to week 20, 2025 were combined with peer-reviewed literature to build a static model estimating burden.
Main results
In the 2024/2025 season, RSV accounted for approximately 105,000 symptomatic cases, 7,500 hospital admissions, and 970 deaths across the Nordic countries.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the continued high burden of RSV alongside other VPRDs as evidence that current immunization efforts remain insufficient, highlighting RSV as a priority for expanded vaccine strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support strengthening adult immunization programs through higher vaccine uptake, broader coverage, and adoption of advanced RSV vaccines to reduce hospitalizations and deaths.
Limitations and evidence strength
The static model relied on national statistics and literature, which may underestimate true burden due to underreporting and limited RSV-specific data, warranting a moderate confidence assessment.

雷贝拉唑对尼帕假病毒和真正的 HCoV-OC43 和 RSV 表现出广谱融合抑制作用

Rabeprazole exhibits broad-spectrum fusion inhibition against Nipah pseudovirus and authentic HCoV-OC43 and RSV
作者： Yunyue Shen, Boxuan Wang, Jie Li, Ze Liang, Lili Liu, Guang Yang, Lei Yan
摘要中文翻译雷贝拉唑此前被定义为一种小分子融合抑制剂,可结合 SARS-CoV-2 刺突的保守疏水性 HR1 凹槽并阻断六螺旋束组装。在这里,我们扩展了其 抗病毒药物 谱,并证明了其对尼帕病毒假型、HCoV-229E 和 RSV 的有效活性。使用 SPR、假病毒中和、对接和 100 ns MD 模拟,我们验证了雷贝拉唑与 NiV F 糖蛋白和 HCoV-229E 刺突的 HR1 结构域的结合,并具有保守的融合抑制机制。临床批准的 PPI 的系统构效关系 (SAR) 分析显示,艾普拉唑和兰索拉唑比雷贝拉唑表现出更强的抗 NiV 效力,而雷贝拉唑硫化物相对于母体化合物表现出增强的 抗病毒药物 活性。雷贝拉唑还以剂量依赖性方式抑制活 RSV 和 HCoV-229E 感染。总的来说,这些发现将 HR1 沟确立为可药物化的泛病毒靶标,并验证基于苯并咪唑的 PPI 作为通用支架,可用于开发口服广谱 抗病毒药物,对抗依赖于 I 类融合机制的系统发育独特的包膜病毒。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在扩大雷贝拉唑(一种已知的 SARS-CoV-2 融合抑制剂)的 抗病毒药物 谱,以包括 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和其他包膜病毒。这项研究的动机是需要广谱 抗病毒药物 来对抗依赖 I 类融合机制的系统发育独特的病毒。
设计与对象
该研究结合了假病毒中和、分子对接和分子动力学模拟进行。它涉及测试雷贝拉唑针对 RSV 和其他相关病毒的作用,重点是其作用机制和 抗病毒药物 效力。
核心方法
研究人员采用SPR(表面等离子体共振)、假病毒中和试验、分子对接模拟和100纳秒MD(分子动力学)模拟来验证雷贝拉唑与NiV F糖蛋白和HCoV-229E刺突的HR1结构域的结合,并评估其抗病毒药物活性。
主要结果
临床批准的 PPI 的系统 SAR(结构-活性关系)分析表明,艾普拉唑和兰索拉唑比雷贝拉唑具有更强的抗 NiV 效力,而与母体化合物相比,硫化雷贝拉唑表现出增强的 抗病毒药物 活性。雷贝拉唑还以剂量依赖性方式抑制活 RSV 和 HCoV-229E 感染。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,雷贝拉唑及其衍生物可以通过保守的融合抑制机制抑制 RSV 和其他包膜病毒,强调 HR1 沟作为广谱 抗病毒药物 的潜在药物靶点。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为开发针对 HR1 沟的口服广谱 抗病毒药物 奠定了基础,这对于对抗引起呼吸道感染的 RSV 和相关病毒可能有价值。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外和假病毒检测,由于缺乏体内测试和临床试验,证据强度中等。这些发现基于结构-活动关系和计算模型,可能无法完全反映现实世界的功效。

Rabeprazole exhibits broad-spectrum fusion inhibition against Nipah pseudovirus and authentic HCoV-OC43 and RSV

Authors: Yunyue Shen, Boxuan Wang, Jie Li, Ze Liang, Lili Liu, Guang Yang, Lei Yan
Original abstract (English)
Rabeprazole was previously defined as a small-molecule fusion inhibitor that binds the conserved hydrophobic HR1 groove of the SARS-CoV-2 spike and blocks six-helix bundle assembly. Here, we expand its antiviral spectrum and demonstrate potent activity against Nipah virus pseudotype, HCoV-229E, and RSV. Using SPR, pseudovirus neutralization, docking, and 100-ns MD simulations, we validated binding of Rabeprazole to the HR1 domains of NiV F glycoprotein and HCoV-229E spike, with a conserved fusion-inhibitory mechanism. Systematic structure-activity relationship (SAR) profiling among clinically approved PPIs revealed that Ilaprazole and Lansoprazole exhibited stronger anti-NiV potency than Rabeprazole, while Rabeprazole sulfide showed enhanced antiviral activity relative to the parent compound. Rabeprazole also inhibited live RSV and HCoV-229E infection in a dose-dependent manner. Collectively, these findings establish the HR1 groove as a druggable pan-viral target and validate benzimidazole-based PPIs as a versatile scaffold for developing orally available, broad-spectrum antivirals against phylogenetically distinct enveloped viruses that rely on class I fusion machinery.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to expand the antiviral spectrum of Rabeprazole, a known SARS-CoV-2 fusion inhibitor, to include Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) and other enveloped viruses. The research was motivated by the need for broad-spectrum antivirals against phylogenetically distinct viruses that rely on class I fusion machinery.
Design and population
The study was conducted using a combination of pseudovirus neutralization, molecular docking, and molecular dynamics simulations. It involved testing Rabeprazole against RSV and other related viruses, with a focus on its mechanism of action and antiviral potency.
Core methods
The researchers employed SPR (Surface Plasmon Resonance), pseudovirus neutralization assays, molecular docking simulations, and 100-ns MD (Molecular Dynamics) simulations to validate the binding of Rabeprazole to the HR1 domains of NiV F glycoprotein and HCoV-229E spike, and to assess its antiviral activity.
Main results
Systematic SAR (Structure-Activity Relationship) profiling among clinically approved PPIs showed that Ilaprazole and Lansoprazole had stronger anti-NiV potency than Rabeprazole, while Rabeprazole sulfide demonstrated enhanced antiviral activity compared to the parent compound. Rabeprazole also inhibited live RSV and HCoV-229E infection in a dose-dependent manner.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that Rabeprazole and its derivatives can inhibit RSV and other enveloped viruses through a conserved fusion-inhibitory mechanism, highlighting the HR1 groove as a potential druggable target for broad-spectrum antivirals.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide a foundation for developing orally available, broad-spectrum antivirals targeting the HR1 groove, which could be valuable for combating RSV and related viruses that cause respiratory infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and pseudovirus assays, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of in vivo testing and clinical trials. The findings are based on structure-activity relationships and computational models, which may not fully reflect real-world efficacy.

60 岁以上成年人 Ad26.RSV.preF–RSV preF 疫苗接种 后 RSV 保护的免疫相关性

Immune correlates of protection against RSV following Ad26.RSV.preF–RSV preF vaccination of adults aged >60 years
作者： Arangassery Rosemary Bastian, Joris Menten, Jeroen Tolboom, Veronica V Rezelj, Mariska G. M. van Rosmalen, Sunita Balla, Jenny Hendriks, Thomas A. Broge, Jishnu Das, Caitlyn H. Linde
摘要中文翻译摘要 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是呼吸道疾病的主要原因,并且没有普遍接受的血清学保护相关性 (CoP)。我们使用研究性 Ad26.RSV.preF–RSV preF 疫苗 评估了 疫苗接种 后年龄 > 60 岁成年人的血清融合前免疫球蛋白 G 结合 (preF-IgG) 和 RSV-A2 中和抗体 (RSV-nAb)。在 疫苗接种 前(第 1 天)和 疫苗接种 后第 15 天测量的 PreF-IgG 和 RSV-nAb 被评估为针对 RSV 介导的急性呼吸道感染 (ARI) 和下呼吸道疾病 (LRTD) 的 CoP。第 15 天时 preF-IgG 和 RSV-nAb 的增加与随访期间 RSV 介导的 ARI 和 LRTD 发生率的减少显着相关。根据受控的 疫苗 功效曲线和 Prentice 标准,这两种标记物均被鉴定为 RSV 介导的 ARI 和 LRTD 的 CoP。这些结果使用第二项随机 3 期疗效试验的样本进行了验证。preF 特异性抗体反应的综合分析表明,preF IgG3 和 IgG2 也可能有助于预防 RSV。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是老年人呼吸道疾病的主要原因,但不存在普遍接受的血清学保护相关性 (CoP)。该研究旨在确定 疫苗接种 后预测对 RSV 介导疾病的保护作用的免疫标记。
设计与对象
该研究评估了接受研究性 Ad26.RSV.preF–RSV preF 疫苗 的 60 岁以上成年人。在 疫苗接种 之前和 疫苗接种 后 15 天测量血清融合前免疫球蛋白 G 结合 (preF-IgG) 和 RSV-A2 中和抗体 (RSV-nAb)。
核心方法
PreF-IgG 和 RSV-nAb 水平被评估为针对 RSV 介导的急性呼吸道感染 (ARI) 和下呼吸道疾病 (LRTD) 的保护作用的相关性。使用受控 疫苗 功效曲线和 Prentice 标准分析这些标志物与疾病结果之间的关联。
主要结果
第 15 天时 preF-IgG 和 RSV-nAb 的增加与随访期间 RSV 介导的 ARI 和 LRTD 发生率的减少显着相关。综合分析表明 preF IgG3 和 IgG2 亚类也可能有助于保护。
解释与创新
作者将 preF-IgG 和 RSV-nAb 确定为抵抗 RSV 介导的 ARI 和 LRTD 的血清学相关物,并使用第二项随机 3 期疗效试验的样本验证了这些标记物。研究进一步表明,特定的 preF IgG 亚类可能会增强保护性免疫。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持使用 preF-IgG 和 RSV-nAb 作为 疫苗 功效的可测量免疫标记物,并可以指导老年人改进 RSV 疫苗 和 疫苗接种 策略的开发。
局限与证据强度
The evidence is limited to abstract-level data and lacks detailed methodological descriptions or numerical effect sizes. While the validation in a phase 3 trial strengthens the findings, the strength of evidence is moderate due to the absence of comprehensive methodological and statistical reporting.

Immune correlates of protection against RSV following Ad26.RSV.preF–RSV preF vaccination of adults aged >60 years

Authors: Arangassery Rosemary Bastian, Joris Menten, Jeroen Tolboom, Veronica V Rezelj, Mariska G. M. van Rosmalen, Sunita Balla, Jenny Hendriks, Thomas A. Broge, Jishnu Das, Caitlyn H. Linde
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of respiratory illness and there is no universally accepted serological correlate of protection (CoP). We evaluated serum pre-fusion immunoglobulin G binding (preF-IgG) and RSV-A2 neutralizing antibodies (RSV-nAb) in adults aged >60 years following vaccination with investigational Ad26.RSV.preF–RSV preF vaccine. PreF-IgG and RSV-nAb measured pre-vaccination (Day 1) and Day 15 post-vaccination were assessed as CoPs against RSV-mediated acute respiratory infection (ARI) and lower respiratory tract disease (LRTD). An increase in both preF-IgG and RSV-nAb at Day 15 was significantly associated with reduced occurrence of RSV-mediated ARI and LRTD during follow-up. Based on controlled vaccine-efficacy curves and Prentice criteria, both markers were identified as CoPs for RSV-mediated ARI and LRTD. These results were validated using samples from a second randomized, phase 3 efficacy trial. Comprehensive profiling of preF-specific antibody responses showed that preF IgG3 and IgG2 may also contribute to protection from RSV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of respiratory illness in older adults, yet no universally accepted serological correlate of protection (CoP) exists. The study aimed to identify immune markers that predict protection against RSV-mediated disease following vaccination.
Design and population
The study evaluated adults aged over 60 years who received the investigational Ad26.RSV.preF–RSV preF vaccine. Serum pre-fusion immunoglobulin G binding (preF-IgG) and RSV-A2 neutralizing antibodies (RSV-nAb) were measured pre-vaccination and 15 days post-vaccination.
Core methods
PreF-IgG and RSV-nAb levels were assessed as correlates of protection against RSV-mediated acute respiratory infection (ARI) and lower respiratory tract disease (LRTD). The association between these markers and disease outcomes was analyzed using controlled vaccine-efficacy curves and Prentice criteria.
Main results
An increase in both preF-IgG and RSV-nAb at Day 15 was significantly associated with reduced occurrence of RSV-mediated ARI and LRTD during follow-up. Comprehensive profiling indicated that preF IgG3 and IgG2 subclasses may also contribute to protection.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors identified preF-IgG and RSV-nAb as serological correlates of protection against RSV-mediated ARI and LRTD, validating these markers using samples from a second randomized phase 3 efficacy trial. The study further suggested that specific preF IgG subclasses may enhance protective immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the use of preF-IgG and RSV-nAb as measurable immune markers for vaccine efficacy and could guide the development of improved RSV vaccines and vaccination strategies in older adults.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to abstract-level data and lacks detailed methodological descriptions or numerical effect sizes. While the validation in a phase 3 trial strengthens the findings, the strength of evidence is moderate due to the absence of comprehensive methodological and statistical reporting.

RSV 的全 基因组 测序和俄罗斯病毒输入的谱系地理学评估

Whole-Genome Sequencing of RSV and Phylogeographic Assessment of Viral Importations into Russia
作者： German V. Roev, Ekaterina V. Pimkina, Dmitry V. Svetlichnyy, Arina V. Peresadina, Maksim I. Nadtoka, Kamil F. Khafizov, Vasiliy G. Akimkin, В. Г. Акимкин
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 引起的下呼吸道感染构成了全球重大公共卫生挑战。使用下一代测序技术可以详细监测病毒遗传变异性,这对于评估免疫预防措施的功效至关重要。在本研究中,对 2021 年 9 月至 2025 年 4 月期间在俄罗斯联邦收集的 106 个样本进行了 RSV 全 基因组 测序。使用了三个 NGS 平台:Illumina MiSeq、Oxford Nanopore Technologies MinION 和 Qitan Tech QNome-3841。使用离散系统发育地理学方法,我们估计分析中包含的 基因组 中至少有 45 个 RSV-A 传入俄罗斯的事件和 39 个 RSV-B 传入俄罗斯的事件。大多数赛事均由俄罗斯选手 基因组 代表。这些结果表明 RSV 经常从国外传入俄罗斯。鉴于可用的基因组样本有限,大多数这些引入与国内可检测到的 传播 无关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 引起的下呼吸道感染是一项重大的全球公共卫生挑战,该研究旨在解决了解 RSV 基因变异和俄罗斯输入动态方面的差距。
设计与对象
对 2021 年 9 月至 2025 年 4 月期间在俄罗斯联邦收集的 106 个样本进行了 RSV 全 基因组 测序,采用离散系统发育地理学方法来评估病毒引入。
核心方法
使用了三个下一代测序平台:Illumina MiSeq、Oxford Nanopore Technologies MinION 和 Qitan Tech QNome-3841,并应用全 基因组 测序来表征 RSV 遗传多样性。
主要结果
据估计,RSV-A 至少有 45 个引入事件,RSV-B 至少有 39 个引入事件,其中大多数事件由单个俄罗斯 基因组 代表,表明来自国外的反复引入。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,RSV 经常从外国来源传入俄罗斯,凸显了 基因组监测 在追踪病毒输入和通报公共卫生应对措施方面的作用。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调了全 基因组 测序对于监测 RSV 遗传变异和输入模式的价值,它可以指导 监测 策略和免疫预防措施。
局限与证据强度
有限的基因组采样限制了在俄罗斯境内对 传播 链的检测,降低了充分描述国内传播特征的能力,并可能低估了传入事件。

Whole-Genome Sequencing of RSV and Phylogeographic Assessment of Viral Importations into Russia

Authors: German V. Roev, Ekaterina V. Pimkina, Dmitry V. Svetlichnyy, Arina V. Peresadina, Maksim I. Nadtoka, Kamil F. Khafizov, Vasiliy G. Akimkin, В. Г. Акимкин
Original abstract (English)
Lower respiratory tract infections caused by the respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) pose a major global public health challenge. The use of next-generation sequencing technologies enables detailed monitoring of viral genetic variability, which is crucial for evaluating the efficacy of immunoprophylactic measures. In this study, whole-genome sequencing of RSV was performed on 106 samples collected in the Russian Federation between September 2021 and April 2025. Three NGS platforms were employed: Illumina MiSeq, Oxford Nanopore Technologies MinION, and Qitan Tech QNome-3841. Using discrete phylogeographic methods, we estimated a minimum of 45 introduction events into Russia for RSV-A and 39 for RSV-B among the genomes included in the analysis. Most events were represented by a single Russian genome. These results indicate recurrent introductions of RSV into Russia from abroad. Given the limited genomic sampling available, most of these introductions were not associated with detectable transmission within the country.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Lower respiratory tract infections caused by respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) represent a major global public health challenge, and the study aimed to address gaps in understanding RSV genetic variability and importation dynamics into Russia.
Design and population
Whole-genome sequencing of RSV was performed on 106 samples collected in the Russian Federation between September 2021 and April 2025, employing discrete phylogeographic methods to assess viral introductions.
Core methods
Three next-generation sequencing platforms were used: Illumina MiSeq, Oxford Nanopore Technologies MinION, and Qitan Tech QNome-3841, with whole-genome sequencing applied to characterize RSV genetic diversity.
Main results
A minimum of 45 introduction events for RSV-A and 39 for RSV-B were estimated, with most events represented by a single Russian genome, indicating recurrent introductions from abroad.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate recurrent RSV introductions into Russia from foreign sources, highlighting the role of genomic surveillance in tracking viral importations and informing public health responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study underscores the value of whole-genome sequencing for monitoring RSV genetic variability and importation patterns, which can guide surveillance strategies and immunoprophylactic measures.
Limitations and evidence strength
The limited genomic sampling restricts detection of transmission chains within Russia, reducing the ability to fully characterize domestic spread and potentially underestimating introduction events.

记录的儿童哮喘诊断与呼吸道病毒活动的双向变化:日本全国范围的中断时间序列分析。

Bidirectional changes in recorded pediatric asthma diagnoses with respiratory virus activity: A nationwide interrupted time-series analysis in Japan.
作者： Naomi Matsumoto, Hisashi Ishida, Shunsaku Hayase, Masanori Ikeda, Takashi Yorifuji
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了呼吸道病毒活动是否会导致儿童哮喘发作,还是仅仅揭示了预先存在的易感性,并指出生命早期的呼吸道感染与随后的哮喘发展有关,但特定病毒的因果作用仍不清楚。
设计与对象
日本利用国家健康保险索赔数据库进行了一项全国性中断时间序列研究,覆盖了约 99% 的人口,每月统计了 2018 年 1 月至 2024 年 3 月期间 19 岁以下儿童新记录的小儿哮喘诊断数量。
核心方法
采用具有两个干预点(2020 年 3 月 NPI 实施和 2023 年 5 月 COVID-19 重新分类)的分段负二项回归来分析哮喘 诊断 趋势,包括年龄分层和性别分层分析,以特应性皮炎作为阴性对照,并对病原体特异性关联进行探索性生态分析。
主要结果
Among 10,508,387 new asthma diagnoses, incidence declined significantly following NPI implementation (incidence rate ratio [IRR] 0.511, 95% CI 0.422–0.620) and rebounded after NPI relaxation (IRR 1.395, 95% CI 1.120–1.737), with the greatest rate differences in infants (0–1 years) at T1 and school-aged children (5–9 years) at T2.
解释与创新
作者将儿科哮喘诊断的双向变化解释为与呼吸道感染(特别是鼻病毒、RSV、流感和肺炎支原体)可能的贡献作用相一致,强调了利用与大流行相关的 NPI 作为评估病毒与哮喘关联的自然实验的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果谨慎地表明,呼吸道病毒活动可能会影响儿童,特别是婴儿和学龄群体的哮喘发作,为 监测 策略和未来对感染介导的哮喘触发因素的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的生态设计和对记录诊断的依赖限制了因果推断,并且在哮喘病例中缺乏直接病原体检测削弱了特异性;由于这些限制,证据强度被评估为中等。

Bidirectional changes in recorded pediatric asthma diagnoses with respiratory virus activity: A nationwide interrupted time-series analysis in Japan.

Authors: Naomi Matsumoto, Hisashi Ishida, Shunsaku Hayase, Masanori Ikeda, Takashi Yorifuji
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Early-life respiratory infections are associated with subsequent asthma development; however, whether respiratory viruses contribute to asthma onset or merely unmask pre-existing susceptibility remains unclear. The COVID-19 pandemic's non-pharmaceutical interventions (NPIs) suppressed respiratory viruses, followed by their resurgence, offering a natural experiment. We examined whether these population-level changes in respiratory virus activity were associated with changes in newly recorded pediatric asthma diagnoses. METHODS: We conducted a nationwide interrupted time-series study using Japan's National Database of Health Insurance Claims, covering approximately 99% of the population. Segmented negative binomial regression with two intervention points-NPI implementation (T1: March 2020) and COVID-19 reclassification (T2: May 2023)-was applied to monthly counts of newly recorded asthma diagnoses among children aged under 19 years (January 2018-March 2024), with atopic dermatitis as negative control. Age-stratified/sex-stratified analyses and exploratory ecological analyses of pathogen-specific associations were conducted. RESULTS: Among 10,508,387 new asthma diagnoses, incidence declined significantly following NPI implementation (incidence rate ratio [IRR] 0.511, 95% confidence interval [CI] 0.422-0.620) and rebounded following NPI relaxation (IRR 1.395, 95% CI:1.120-1.737). Atopic dermatitis showed attenuated changes at both points. Rate differences were greatest in infants (0-1 years) at T1 and school-aged children (5-9 years) at T2. Rhinovirus, respiratory syncytial virus, influenza, and Mycoplasma pneumoniae, but not SARS-CoV-2, correlated with asthma diagnoses in ecological analyses. CONCLUSIONS: Newly recorded pediatric asthma diagnoses showed bidirectional changes that paralleled respiratory virus suppression and resurgence, compatible with a possible contributory role of respiratory infections, particularly among infants and school-aged children.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed whether respiratory virus activity contributes to pediatric asthma onset or merely unmasks pre-existing susceptibility, noting that early-life respiratory infections are linked to subsequent asthma development but the causal role of specific viruses remains unclear.
Design and population
A nationwide interrupted time-series study was conducted in Japan using the National Database of Health Insurance Claims, covering approximately 99% of the population, with monthly counts of newly recorded pediatric asthma diagnoses among children under 19 years from January 2018 to March 2024.
Core methods
Segmented negative binomial regression with two intervention points—NPI implementation in March 2020 and COVID-19 reclassification in May 2023—was applied to analyze asthma diagnosis trends, including age-stratified and sex-stratified analyses, with atopic dermatitis as a negative control and exploratory ecological analyses of pathogen-specific associations.
Main results
Among 10,508,387 new asthma diagnoses, incidence declined significantly following NPI implementation (incidence rate ratio [IRR] 0.511, 95% CI 0.422–0.620) and rebounded after NPI relaxation (IRR 1.395, 95% CI 1.120–1.737), with the greatest rate differences in infants (0–1 years) at T1 and school-aged children (5–9 years) at T2.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the bidirectional changes in pediatric asthma diagnoses as compatible with a possible contributory role of respiratory infections, particularly rhinovirus, RSV, influenza, and Mycoplasma pneumoniae, highlighting the novelty of leveraging pandemic-related NPIs as a natural experiment to assess virus-asthma associations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings cautiously suggest that respiratory virus activity may influence asthma onset in children, particularly infants and school-aged groups, informing surveillance strategies and future research into infection-mediated asthma triggers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s ecological design and reliance on recorded diagnoses limit causal inference, and the absence of direct pathogen detection in asthma cases weakens specificity; the evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to these limitations.

怀孕期间服用二价 RSVpreF 疫苗 (Abrysvo®) 的安全性概况:2025 年在巴拿马进行的一项活跃的真实世界药物警戒研究

Safety Profile of the Bivalent RSVpreF Vaccine (Abrysvo®) Administered During Pregnancy: An Active Real-World Pharmacovigilance Study in Panama, 2025
作者： Ana Lucas Quintero, Rodrigo Deantonio-Suárez, Isaac Fernández Camargo, Ana Peralta Alvarado, Luis Escobar, Belén Ricci Pérez, Luis Flores, Cynthia Gadea, Sofía Barrios, Emmanuel Guillén
摘要中文翻译背景/目标。呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)是婴儿急性下呼吸道感染的重要原因。巴拿马于 2025 年 7 月引入了二价预输液 F 疫苗 (RSVpreF),用于妊娠 32.0 至 36.6 周之间的给药。在这个热带环境中,呼吸道病毒全年都会检测到,RSV 活性在雨季期间增加。我们对自我报告的孕产妇和新生儿事件和结果进行了描述,以生成来自中美洲的早期引入后真实世界安全证据。这项回顾性描述性观察研究包括 2025 年 7 月至 12 月期间在妊娠 32.0 至 36.6 周期间接种 RSVpreF 的 18 岁或以上女性,并于 2026 年 1 月至 7 月期间招募。采用了结构化电话、虚拟或面对面访谈。我们计算了绝对频率和相对频率以及 95% 威尔逊置信区间 (CI)。在收到的 225 份问卷中,有 200 份符合分析条件。产妇平均年龄为 28.1 岁 (SD 6.4)。55.5% 的参与者至少报告了一种反应,主要是本地参与者;注射部位疼痛最常见(48.0%)。17.5% 的人报告至少一种产科疾病,包括妊娠期高血压疾病 (12.0%)。早产率为 2.0%,低出生体重率为 2.5%。没有胎儿或新生儿死亡、血小板减少、瘫痪、面瘫或吉兰-巴利综合征的报告。黄疸是最常见的新生儿发病率(20.0%)。在此 队列研究 中,自我报告的反应原性主要是局部的,并且没有明显的意外事件群。参与者的选择、回顾性自我报告、缺乏未接种疫苗的比较者以及样本量限制了风险估计和不常见事件的检测。研究结果提供了来自中美洲的早期主动药物警戒证据,并支持继续进行 监测 临床验证的孕产妇和新生儿结局。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿严重急性下呼吸道感染的主要原因,因此需要母亲 疫苗接种 来保护新生儿。该研究旨在为巴拿马怀孕期间施用二价 RSVpreF 疫苗 (Abrysvo®) 提供早期引入后的真实安全证据。
设计与对象
这项回顾性描述性观察研究招募了 2025 年 7 月至 12 月在巴拿马妊娠 32.0 至 36.6 周期间接受 RSVpreF 疫苗 的 18 岁或以上女性,招募时间为 2026 年 1 月至 7 月。
核心方法
使用结构化电话、虚拟或面对面访谈来收集自我报告的孕产妇和新生儿事件和结果。计算反应原性和产科条件的绝对频率和相对频率(95% Wilson 置信区间)。
主要结果
在 200 名符合条件的参与者中,55.5% 的人报告至少一种反应,主要是局部反应,48.0% 的人报告注射部位疼痛。产科疾病发生率为 17.5%,其中妊娠期高血压疾病发生率为 12.0%。早产率为 2.0%,低出生体重率为 2.5%。没有胎儿或新生儿死亡、血小板减少、瘫痪、面瘫或吉兰-巴利综合征的报告。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为主要显示局部反应原性,没有意外的集群事件,提供了来自中美洲的早期积极药物警戒证据。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持继续进行 监测 临床验证的孕产妇和新生儿结局,以监测 疫苗 在现实环境中的安全性。
局限与证据强度
参与者的选择、回顾性自我报告、缺乏未接种疫苗的比较者以及有限的样本量限制了风险评估和不常见事件的检测,需要进行适度的置信度评估。

Safety Profile of the Bivalent RSVpreF Vaccine (Abrysvo®) Administered During Pregnancy: An Active Real-World Pharmacovigilance Study in Panama, 2025

Authors: Ana Lucas Quintero, Rodrigo Deantonio-Suárez, Isaac Fernández Camargo, Ana Peralta Alvarado, Luis Escobar, Belén Ricci Pérez, Luis Flores, Cynthia Gadea, Sofía Barrios, Emmanuel Guillén
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives. Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is an important cause of acute lower respiratory infection in infants. Panama introduced the bivalent prefusion F vaccine (RSVpreF) for administration between 32.0 and 36.6 weeks of gestation in July 2025. In this tropical setting, respiratory viruses are detected throughout the year, with RSV activity increasing during the rainy season. We characterized self-reported maternal and neonatal events and outcomes to generate early post-introduction real-world safety evidence from Central America. This retrospective descriptive observational study included women aged 18 years or older vaccinated with RSVpreF between 32.0 and 36.6 weeks of gestation from July through December 2025 and recruited from January through July 2026. Structured telephone, virtual, or in-person interviews were used. We calculated absolute and relative frequencies and 95% Wilson confidence intervals (CIs). Of 225 questionnaires obtained, 200 were eligible for analysis. Mean maternal age was 28.1 years (SD 6.4). At least one reaction was reported by 55.5% of participants, mainly local; injection-site pain was most frequent (48.0%). At least one obstetric condition was reported by 17.5%, including hypertensive disorders of pregnancy (12.0%). Preterm birth occurred in 2.0% and low birth weight in 2.5%. No fetal or neonatal deaths, thrombocytopenia, paralysis, facial paralysis, or Guillain–Barré syndrome were reported. Jaundice was the most frequent neonatal morbidity (20.0%). In this cohort, self-reported reactogenicity was mainly local, and no unexpected cluster of events was apparent. Participant selection, retrospective self-report, absence of an unvaccinated comparator, and sample size limit risk estimation and detection of uncommon events. The findings provide early active-pharmacovigilance evidence from Central America and support continued surveillance with clinically verified maternal and neonatal outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of severe acute lower respiratory infection in infants, creating a need for maternal vaccination to protect newborns. The study aimed to generate early post-introduction real-world safety evidence on the bivalent RSVpreF vaccine (Abrysvo®) administered during pregnancy in Panama.
Design and population
This retrospective descriptive observational study enrolled women aged 18 years or older who received the RSVpreF vaccine between 32.0 and 36.6 weeks of gestation in Panama from July through December 2025, with recruitment conducted from January through July 2026.
Core methods
Structured telephone, virtual, or in-person interviews were used to collect self-reported maternal and neonatal events and outcomes. Absolute and relative frequencies with 95% Wilson confidence intervals were calculated for reactogenicity and obstetric conditions.
Main results
Among 200 eligible participants, 55.5% reported at least one reaction, primarily local with injection-site pain in 48.0%. Obstetric conditions occurred in 17.5%, including hypertensive disorders of pregnancy in 12.0%. Preterm birth occurred in 2.0% and low birth weight in 2.5%. No fetal or neonatal deaths, thrombocytopenia, paralysis, facial paralysis, or Guillain–Barré syndrome were reported.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as showing mainly local reactogenicity with no unexpected clusters of events, providing early active pharmacovigilance evidence from Central America.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support continued surveillance with clinically verified maternal and neonatal outcomes to monitor vaccine safety in real-world settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
Participant selection, retrospective self-report, absence of an unvaccinated comparator, and limited sample size constrain risk estimation and detection of uncommon events, warranting a moderate confidence assessment.

2023-2025年河北省急性呼吸道感染病原学构成及流行病学特征

Etiological composition and epidemiological characteristics of acute respiratory infections in Hebei Province, 2023–2025
作者： Nana Guo, Ziyi Pang, Yan Li, Caixiao Jiang, Minghao Geng, Wentao Wu, Guangyue Han, Han Xu, Qi Li, Xu Han
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
急性呼吸道感染(ARTI)给全球公共卫生带来了重大负担,而在本研究之前,对河北省常见呼吸道病原体的病原学组成和流行病学模式的描述还不够充分。
设计与对象
一项前瞻性 监测 研究纳入了 2023 年 10 月至 2025 年 9 月河北省 11,942 名患有流感样疾病 (ILI) 或严重急性呼吸道感染 (SARI) 的门诊和住院患者。
核心方法
收集咽拭子、痰液和支气管肺泡灌洗液,并使用多重PCR试剂盒进行筛查;分析了年龄分布、时间趋势和合并感染模式。
主要结果
SARI 病例中病原体阳性率为 45.71%,而 ILI 病例中病原体阳性率为 41.62%;呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 在0-4岁SARI组中占主导地位,并且RSV表现出冬春季节的主要活性。
解释与创新
该研究确定 RSV 是幼儿 SARI 的主要原因,并强调了其季节性冬春季优势,为有针对性的预防策略提供了新的当地证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持在儿科 SARI 中优先考虑 RSV 监测 和预防,特别是在 0-4 岁年龄组,并且可以指导当地的免疫和感染控制政策。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计和对多重 PCR 的依赖(没有验证性测序)限制了病因解析和因果推断;证据强度中等。

Etiological composition and epidemiological characteristics of acute respiratory infections in Hebei Province, 2023–2025

Authors: Nana Guo, Ziyi Pang, Yan Li, Caixiao Jiang, Minghao Geng, Wentao Wu, Guangyue Han, Han Xu, Qi Li, Xu Han
Original abstract (English)
Background Acute respiratory tract infections (ARTIs) are a major global public health concern. This study aimed to characterize the pathogen spectrum and epidemiological features of 11 common respiratory pathogens among outpatients and hospitalized patients in Hebei Province. Methods A total of 11,942 patients with influenza-like illness (ILI) or severe acute respiratory infection (SARI) were enrolled from October 2023 to September 2025. Throat swabs, sputum, and bronchoalveolar lavage fluid were collected and screened using a multiplex PCR kit. Age distribution, temporal trends, and coinfection patterns were analyzed. Results The results showed that the pathogen positivity rate among SARI (45.71%) was higher than that among ILI (41.62%); influenza virus (IFV) had the highest detection rate among ILI, whilst Mycoplasma pneumoniae (M. pneumoniae) was the most frequently detected pathogen among SARI; In terms of age distribution: IFV had the highest detection rate across all age groups in ILI; in the 0–4-year-old SARI group, respiratory syncytial virus was predominant; in the 5–14-year-old group, the detection rate of M. pneumoniae reached 43.44 %; and in the group aged 15 years and over, IFV and Severe Acute Respiratory Syndrome Coronavirus 2(SARS-CoV-2) were the predominant pathogens. SARS-CoV-2 were intermittently detected throughout the year, with three waves of epidemic peaks observed. IFV and RSV predominantly emerged during winter and spring, while rhinovirus showed higher activity in summer and autumn. pneumoniae exhibited a high incidence rate initially, which has been declining steadily since the end of 2024.The proportion of multi-pathogen co-infections among positive specimens was 14.88%, the proportion of co-infections among SARI (23.76 %) was significantly higher than that among ILI (9.28 %). pneumoniae and rhinovirus was the most common, followed by human bocavirus, parainfluenza virus and enterovirus, which were also among the pathogens frequently detected in combination. Conclusion This study elucidated the epidemiological characteristics of respiratory pathogens in Hebei Province, providing a scientific basis for the precise prevention and control of ARTIs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Acute respiratory tract infections (ARTIs) impose a major global public health burden, and the etiological composition and epidemiological patterns of common respiratory pathogens in Hebei Province were insufficiently characterized before this study.
Design and population
A prospective surveillance study enrolled 11,942 outpatients and hospitalized patients with influenza-like illness (ILI) or severe acute respiratory infection (SARI) from October 2023 to September 2025 across Hebei Province.
Core methods
Throat swabs, sputum, and bronchoalveolar lavage fluid were collected and screened using a multiplex PCR kit; age distribution, temporal trends, and coinfection patterns were analyzed.
Main results
Among SARI cases the pathogen positivity rate was 45.71% versus 41.62% in ILI; respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) was predominant in the 0–4-year-old SARI group, and RSV showed predominant winter–spring activity.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified RSV as a leading cause of SARI in young children and highlighted its seasonal winter–spring predominance, providing new local evidence to inform targeted prevention strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support prioritizing RSV surveillance and prevention in pediatric SARI, particularly in the 0–4-year age group, and can guide local immunization and infection-control policies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s cross-sectional design and reliance on multiplex PCR without confirmatory sequencing limit etiological resolution and causal inference; evidence strength is moderate.

未反应的 RSV 聚合酶结构扩展了核苷酸添加循环的后转位景观

Unreacted RSV Polymerase Structures Expand the Post-Translocation Landscape of the Nucleotide Addition Cycle
作者： Dongdong Cao, Eleanore Zhou, Jun Sim, Bo Liang, 심준
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) RNA 合成依赖于 RNA 依赖性 RNA 聚合酶,这是一种由 L 和 P 蛋白组成的复合物。我们最近解决了 RSV 核苷酸添加循环 (NAC) 的四种主要结构状态,建立了病毒 RNA 延伸的框架。然而,反应衍生的复合物受到催化效率和中间占有率的影响,并且可能不完全采样 NAC 构象景观。在这里,我们报告了两种未反应的 RSV 聚合酶与易位后 RNA 产物模拟物结合的冷冻电子显微镜 (cryo-EM) 结构。一种结构紧密地概括了先前确定的反应衍生的易位后状态,表明可以独立于核苷酸掺入来恢复规范结构。第二种结构揭示了一个额外的易位后中间体,它保留了相同的RNA寄存器和空的+1产物结合位点,同时采用双结构域L组织。结构比较进一步确定了催化中心附近的局部重排,包括基序 A 环和支持螺旋的变化。总之,这些发现扩展了 RSV 易位后阶段的结构景观并完善了当前的 NAC 模型。更广泛地说,我们的结果强调未反应的状态匹配复合物作为反应衍生结构的补充方法,用于捕获动态反应循环的更完整的结构快照。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) RNA 合成依赖于 RNA 依赖性 RNA 聚合酶,这是一种由 L 和 P 蛋白组成的复合物。该研究旨在扩大对 RSV 核苷酸添加循环 (NAC) 易位后阶段的结构理解。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用冷冻电子显微镜 (cryo-EM) 对未反应的 RSV 聚合酶进行结构分析,以捕获易位后 RNA 产物模拟结合状态。该研究是在实验室环境中进行的,重点是 RSV 聚合酶复合物。
核心方法
研究人员使用冷冻电子显微镜 (cryo-EM) 确定了与易位后 RNA 产物模拟物结合的未反应 RSV 聚合酶的两种结构。进行结构比较以确定催化中心附近的局部重排。
主要结果
报道了与易位后 RNA 产物模拟物结合的未反应 RSV 聚合酶的两种冷冻电镜结构。一种结构紧密地概括了先前确定的反应衍生的易位后状态,而第二种结构则揭示了具有双结构域 L 组织的附加中间体。
解释与创新
这些发现扩展了 RSV 易位后阶段的结构景观并完善了当前的 NAC 模型。该研究强调未反应的状态匹配复合物作为反应衍生结构的补充方法,用于捕获动态反应循环的更完整的结构快照。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 RSV 聚合酶结构动力学的深入了解,这可以为针对 RNA 合成的 抗病毒药物 策略的开发提供信息。它们还有助于更好地了解病毒复制过程。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于结构分析,不包括功能或临床数据。该证据基于冷冻电镜结构,由于缺乏直接的生化或生物学验证,因此被认为强度中等。

Unreacted RSV Polymerase Structures Expand the Post-Translocation Landscape of the Nucleotide Addition Cycle

Authors: Dongdong Cao, Eleanore Zhou, Jun Sim, Bo Liang, 심준
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) RNA synthesis relies on RNA-dependent RNA polymerase, a complex composed of the L and P proteins. We recently resolved four major structural states of the RSV nucleotide addition cycle (NAC), establishing a framework for viral RNA elongation. However, reaction-derived complexes are influenced by catalytic efficiency and intermediate occupancy and may incompletely sample the NAC conformational landscape. Here, we report two cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) structures of unreacted RSV polymerase bound to a post-translocation RNA product mimic. One structure closely recapitulates the previously determined reaction-derived post-translocation state, demonstrating that the canonical architecture can be recovered independently of nucleotide incorporation. The second structure reveals an additional post-translocation intermediate that retains the same RNA register and vacant +1 product-binding site while adopting a two-domain L organization. Structural comparisons further identify localized rearrangements near the catalytic center, including changes in the motif A loop and supporting helix. Together, these findings expand the structural landscape of the RSV post-translocation stage and refine the current NAC model. More broadly, our results highlight unreacted state-matched complexes as a complementary approach to reaction-derived structures for capturing more complete structural snapshots of dynamic reaction cycles.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) RNA synthesis relies on RNA-dependent RNA polymerase, a complex composed of the L and P proteins. The study aimed to expand the structural understanding of the post-translocation stage of the nucleotide addition cycle (NAC) in RSV.
Design and population
The study involved structural analysis of unreacted RSV polymerase using cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) to capture post-translocation RNA product mimic-bound states. The research was conducted in a laboratory setting and focused on the RSV polymerase complex.
Core methods
The researchers used cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) to determine two structures of unreacted RSV polymerase bound to a post-translocation RNA product mimic. Structural comparisons were performed to identify localized rearrangements near the catalytic center.
Main results
Two cryo-EM structures of unreacted RSV polymerase bound to a post-translocation RNA product mimic were reported. One structure closely recapitulates the previously determined reaction-derived post-translocation state, while the second reveals an additional intermediate with a two-domain L organization.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings expand the structural landscape of the RSV post-translocation stage and refine the current NAC model. The study highlights unreacted state-matched complexes as a complementary approach to reaction-derived structures for capturing more complete structural snapshots of dynamic reaction cycles.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the structural dynamics of RSV polymerase, which could inform the development of antiviral strategies targeting RNA synthesis. They also contribute to a better understanding of the viral replication process.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to structural analysis and does not include functional or clinical data. The evidence is based on cryo-EM structures and is considered moderate in strength due to the absence of direct biochemical or biological validation.
