流行病学,32,125 名儿童中 13 种呼吸道病原体的时间趋势和合并感染:一项为期 3 年的回顾性研究

Epidemiology, temporal trends and co-infection of 13 respiratory pathogens in 32,125 children: a 3-year retrospective study
作者： Yu Zhang, Siyu Wang, Xiaoming Huang
摘要中文翻译背景 小儿急性呼吸道感染给全球带来沉重的疾病负担。明确大流行后的流行病学特征,指导临床管理和公共卫生预防。方法回顾性研究2023年至2025年在华西第二大学医院接受13种病原体多重PCR检测的18岁以下患者。提取实验室数据并计算病原体阳性率、单一/合并感染比例。完成了按年份、季节、年龄和性别的分层分析以及合并感染模式的统计整理。结果 总共纳入了 32,125 名儿童。总病原阳性率达63.4%,其中单一感染49.8%,以双重感染为主的合并感染13.6%。人类鼻病毒(22.9%)排名第一,其次是呼吸道合胞病毒(15.2%)。病原菌随季节、年龄变化明显:春、秋季HRV升高;夏季流行副流感、腺病毒、冠状病毒;合胞病毒和流感在冬季达到高峰。呼吸道合胞病毒 主要影响婴儿;鼻病毒和副流感在幼儿和学龄前儿童中普遍存在;肺炎支原体随着年龄的增长而增加,并在学龄儿童中达到顶峰。双重感染几乎占据了所有的合并感染;最常见的非流感组合是 HRSV-HRV 和 HRV-HAdV。三种或更多病原体的感染很少见。结论 HRV和HRSV为主儿童呼吸道病原菌,具有明显的季节性和年龄聚集性。大多数合并感染发生在冬季和幼儿。HRV 检测需要对潜在的无症状携带者进行仔细解释。当地流行病学特征应告知针对年龄和季节的 诊断 和包括 HRSV 预防在内的有针对性的预防。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
小儿急性呼吸道感染给全球带来了巨大的疾病负担,需要明确大流行后的流行病学特征来指导临床管理和公共卫生预防。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究纳入了 2023 年至 2025 年在华西第二大学医院接受 13 种病原体多重 PCR 检测的 32,125 名 18 岁以下患者。
核心方法
提取并分析实验室数据,计算病原体阳性率、单一感染和合并感染比例,并按年份、季节、年龄和性别进行分层分析。
主要结果
病原菌总阳性率为63.4%,其中单一感染49.8%,合并感染13.6%,其中双重感染几乎全部是合并感染;人鼻病毒(22.9%)和呼吸道合胞病毒(15.2%)是最常见的病原体。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,人鼻病毒和 呼吸道合胞病毒 在儿科呼吸道病原体中占主导地位,具有明显的季节性和年龄相关聚类,这凸显了由于潜在的无症状携带而需要仔细解释鼻病毒检测的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
当地流行病学模式应告知针对年龄和季节的 诊断 和预防策略,包括有针对性的 呼吸道合胞病毒 预防。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对多重 PCR 结果的依赖而受到限制,可能无法区分无症状携带者和活动性感染,从而降低了因果推断的确定性。

Epidemiology, temporal trends and co-infection of 13 respiratory pathogens in 32,125 children: a 3-year retrospective study

Authors: Yu Zhang, Siyu Wang, Xiaoming Huang
Original abstract (English)
Background Pediatric acute respiratory tract infections bring heavy global disease burden. Clarifying post-pandemic epidemiological features guides clinical management and public health prevention. Methods This retrospective study enrolled patients under 18 years receiving 13-pathogen multiplex PCR at West China Second University Hospital from 2023 to 2025. We extracted laboratory data and calculated pathogen positivity, single/co-infection proportions. Stratified analyses by year, season, age and sex and statistical collation of co-infection patterns were completed. Results In total, 32,125 children were included. The total pathogen positive rate reached 63.4%, including 49.8% single infection and 13.6% co-infection dominated by dual infection. Human rhinovirus (22.9%) ranked first, followed by respiratory syncytial virus (15.2%). Pathogens varied obviously across seasons and ages: HRV surged in spring and autumn; parainfluenza, adenovirus and coronavirus prevailed in summer; syncytial virus and influenza peaked in winter. Respiratory syncytial virus mainly affected infants; rhinovirus and parainfluenza were prevalent in toddlers and preschoolers; Mycoplasma pneumoniae rose with age and peaked among school-age children. Dual infection occupied nearly all co-infections; the top non-influenza combinations were HRSV–HRV and HRV–HAdV. Infections with three or more pathogens were rare. Conclusions HRV and HRSV dominated pediatric respiratory pathogens with obvious seasonal and age clustering. Most co-infections occurred in winter and young kids. HRV detection requires careful explanation for potential asymptomatic carriage. Local epidemiological characteristics should inform age-and seasonal-targeted diagnosis and targeted prevention including HRSV prevention.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Pediatric acute respiratory tract infections impose a substantial global disease burden, and clarifying post-pandemic epidemiological features is needed to guide clinical management and public health prevention.
Design and population
This retrospective study enrolled 32,125 patients under 18 years who underwent 13-pathogen multiplex PCR testing at West China Second University Hospital from 2023 to 2025.
Core methods
Laboratory data were extracted and analyzed to calculate pathogen positivity, single and co-infection proportions, with stratified analyses by year, season, age and sex.
Main results
The total pathogen positive rate was 63.4%, including 49.8% single infections and 13.6% co-infections, with dual infections comprising nearly all co-infections; human rhinovirus (22.9%) and respiratory syncytial virus (15.2%) were the most prevalent pathogens.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that human rhinovirus and respiratory syncytial virus dominated pediatric respiratory pathogens with clear seasonal and age-related clustering, highlighting the need for careful interpretation of rhinovirus detection due to potential asymptomatic carriage.
Scientific and public-health significance
Local epidemiological patterns should inform age- and season-targeted diagnosis and prevention strategies, including targeted respiratory syncytial virus prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on multiplex PCR results, which may not distinguish asymptomatic carriage from active infection, reducing the certainty of causal inference.

晚期早产和小于胎龄——风险加倍?

Late preterm and small for gestational age – double the risk?
作者： Anne Greenough, Allan Jenkinson, Ann Hickey
摘要中文翻译妊娠 34 + 0 周之后和 37 + 0 周之前出生的晚期早产儿占所有早产儿的 75% 以上,因此他们可能出现的任何问题都会带来相当大的医疗负担。人们越来越认识到,与足月出生的婴儿相比,他们可能遭受更严重的急性和慢性呼吸道疾病。在一项对 1022 名婴儿进行的单中心研究中,胎龄 34、35 和 36 周出生的婴儿需要呼吸支持的比例分别为 32%、18% 和 11%,需要辅助管饲的比例分别为 55%、24% 和 8%。1 在 1998 年至 2016 年间出生的 1,650,450 名妊娠 35 至 41 周的婴儿中,呼吸道、新生儿重症、感染和神经系统疾病的风险在 35 周时最高,并逐渐下降,直至 39 周。2 结果受种族影响。在我们的单中心研究中,黑人晚期早产儿较少需要有创和无创通气,并且住院时间 (LOS) 较短。3 晚期早产儿的再入院率较高。4 他们在 18 个月大时感染 RSV 和哮喘的风险增加,在学龄期出现更多喘息和夜间咳嗽的风险。5 在 1973 年至 2013-4 年间出生于瑞典的 4,079,878 名单身人士的全国 队列研究 中,其中 14.9% 被诊断患有哮喘,晚期早产儿的哮喘风险增加了 1.31 倍。6 在随访中,他们的肺功能也可能较差,7 尤其是在被动吸烟的情况下。在三到七岁时,晚产儿的强迫振荡测量结果更差,表明外周气道阻力增加,特别是如果他们因肺部感染住院并接触过被动烟雾。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了与晚期早产儿相关的医疗负担和发病率,晚期早产儿占早产儿的 75% 以上,与足月儿相比,他们面临更高的呼吸道并发症风险。
设计与对象
该研究综合了多个 队列研究 的数据,其中包括 1998 年至 2016 年期间出生的 1,650,450 名妊娠 35 至 41 周之间出生的婴儿的全国 队列研究,以及 1973 年至 2013-4 年间出生的 4,079,878 名瑞典 队列研究 的数据。
核心方法
该研究使用人口水平数据集分析了不同孕龄的发病风险,结果按孕周分层,并根据种族等因素进行调整。
主要结果
呼吸道疾病、严重新生儿疾病、感染性疾病和神经系统疾病的风险在妊娠 35 周时最高,并逐渐下降,直至 39 周。与足月儿相比,晚期早产儿患哮喘的风险增加 1.31。
解释与创新
研究结果强调,晚期早产与呼吸系统和神经系统疾病(包括哮喘)风险升高相关,其中在妊娠 35 周时观察到的负担最高。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调,晚期早产儿(特别是 35 周出生的早产儿)需要有针对性的 监测 和预防策略,以减轻长期呼吸和神经并发症。
局限与证据强度
一些分析的证据受到单中心数据的限制,以及种族和被动吸烟暴露的潜在混淆,需要对研究结果进行谨慎解释。

Late preterm and small for gestational age – double the risk?

Authors: Anne Greenough, Allan Jenkinson, Ann Hickey
Original abstract (English)
Late preterm infants born after 34 + 0 and before 37 + 0 weeks of gestation account for more than 75% of all preterm births so any problems they may have represent a sizeable healthcare burden. It is increasingly recognised that they can suffer greater acute and chronic respiratory morbidity than infants born at term. In a single centre study of 1022 infants, the need for respiratory support was 32%,18% and 11% in infants born at 34, 35 and 36 weeks of gestational age respectively and for supplemental tube feeding was 55%, 24% and 8% respectively. 1 Amongst 1,650,450 infants born between 35 and 41 weeks of gestation between 1998 and 2016, the risks for respiratory, severe neonatal, infectious and neurological morbidities were highest at 35 weeks and gradually decreased until 39 weeks. 2 The results are influenced by ethnicity. In our single centre study Black late preterm infants less frequently needed invasive and non-invasive ventilation and had a shorter duration of length of stay (LOS). 3 Late preterm infants have a higher rate of readmission. 4 They have an increased risk of RSV infection and asthma at 18months and more wheeze and nocturnal cough at school age. 5 In a national cohort of 4,079,878 singletons born in Sweden between 1973 and 2013-4, of whom 14.9% were diagnosed with asthma, the late preterm individuals had 1.31 increased risk of asthma. 6 They also may have poorer lung function at follow-up, 7 particularly if exposed to passive smoking. At three to seven years those born late preterm had worse forced oscillometry results indicating increased peripheral airways resistance, particularly if they had been hospitalised for pulmonary infection and had passive smoke exposure.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the healthcare burden and morbidity associated with late preterm infants, who constitute over 75% of preterm births and face higher risks of respiratory complications compared to term infants.
Design and population
The research synthesizes data from multiple cohorts, including a national cohort of 1,650,450 infants born between 35 and 41 weeks of gestation from 1998 to 2016, and a Swedish cohort of 4,079,878 singletons born between 1973 and 2013-4.
Core methods
The study analyzed morbidity risks across gestational ages using population-level datasets, with outcomes stratified by gestational week and adjusted for factors such as ethnicity.
Main results
Risks for respiratory, severe neonatal, infectious, and neurological morbidities were highest at 35 weeks of gestation and gradually decreased until 39 weeks. Late preterm infants had a 1.31 increased risk of asthma compared to term infants.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight that late preterm birth is associated with elevated risks of respiratory and neurological morbidities, including asthma, with the highest burden observed at 35 weeks of gestation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results underscore the need for targeted surveillance and preventive strategies for late preterm infants, particularly those born at 35 weeks, to mitigate long-term respiratory and neurological complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by single-centre data for some analyses and potential confounding by ethnicity and passive smoke exposure, warranting cautious interpretation of the findings.

福州市儿童呼吸道病原体五年监测(2019-2024):COVID-19大流行的影响

Five-year surveillance of pediatric respiratory pathogens in Fuzhou (2019–2024): impact of the COVID-19 pandemic
作者： Ting Liu, Danna Chen, Songcan Huang, Qingkeng Lin, Jianqin Lin, Qianqian Chen, Lin Cai
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在调查2019年至2024年福州市儿童呼吸道病原体的流行病学特征和分布模式,特别关注与COVID-19大流行相关的变化。这项回顾性研究纳入了2019年6月至2024年5月期间在福建省福州儿童医院因急性呼吸道感染住院的0-18岁儿童。收集鼻拭子标本,采用多重PCR检测11种呼吸道病原体。分析病原体在年龄组、性别、季节和流行阶段的分布特征。共纳入37,724份鼻拭子样本,其中男性22,327人,女性15,397人。总体检测阳性率为58.78%。鼻病毒阳性率最高(20.95%),其次是呼吸道合胞病毒(11.12%)和肺炎支原体(6.89%)。8.08% 的标本中发现了双重感染。多变量逻辑回归揭示了三种最常见病原体的不同年龄、性别和季节模式:肺炎支原体风险随着年龄的增长而逐渐增加(≥ 6 岁:OR = 6.100),RSV 在婴儿中占主导地位,而鼻病毒则表现出不太明显的年龄模式。在四个大流行阶段(大流行前、严格的 NPI、过渡和限制后),病原体检测表现出不同的轨迹。肺炎疫情期间大幅下降,限购后回升(从1.11%上升至12.61%);鼻病毒在严格的 NPI 期间有所下降,但迅速反弹并保持稳定;在严格的 NPI 期间,RSV 反而增加(从 10.66% 增加到 26.74%)。这项研究提供了福州儿童呼吸道病原体 流行病学 的证据,揭示了不同流行阶段检测模式的病原体特异性变化。这些发现强调了大流行后时代持续的 监测 和针对病原体的预防策略的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了了解 COVID-19 大流行如何改变福州儿童呼吸道病原体 流行病学 的需要,重点关注病原体特异性变化以及对其在儿童中分布模式的了解差距。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究纳入了 2019 年 6 月至 2024 年 5 月期间在福州儿童医院因急性呼吸道感染住院的 37,724 名 0-18 岁儿童,其中男性 22,327 名,女性 15,397 名。
核心方法
使用多重 PCR 检测鼻拭子样本中的 11 种呼吸道病原体,并应用多变量逻辑回归分析最常见病原体的年龄、性别和季节模式。
主要结果
RSV 总体阳性率为 11.12%,多变量分析显示 RSV 在婴儿中占主导地位;RSV 检测在严格的非药物干预期间反而增加(从 10.66% 增加到 26.74%),而在限制后则下降。
解释与创新
作者将严格的 NPI 期间 RSV 的非典型上升解释为早期接触减少导致延迟感染的潜在后果,强调了福州之前未详述的病原体特异性大流行影响。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持大流行后时代的针对性 RSV 监测 和预防策略,特别是对于在非典型循环期间仍处于最高风险的婴儿。
局限与证据强度
该研究的单中心回顾性设计和对医院抽样的依赖限制了对社区环境的普遍适用性,需要对 RSV 特定趋势进行谨慎解释。

Five-year surveillance of pediatric respiratory pathogens in Fuzhou (2019–2024): impact of the COVID-19 pandemic

Authors: Ting Liu, Danna Chen, Songcan Huang, Qingkeng Lin, Jianqin Lin, Qianqian Chen, Lin Cai
Original abstract (English)
This study aims to investigate the epidemiological characteristics and distribution patterns of respiratory pathogens in children in Fuzhou between 2019 and 2024, with a particular focus on changes associated with the COVID-19 pandemic. This retrospective study included children aged 0–18 years hospitalized for acute respiratory infections in Fuzhou Children’s Hospital of Fujian Province between June 2019 and May 2024. Nasal swab specimens were collected to test for 11 respiratory pathogens using multiplex PCR. The distribution characteristics of pathogens were analyzed across age groups, genders, seasons, and pandemic phases. A total of 37,724 nasal swab specimens were included, comprising 22,327 males and 15,397 females. The overall test positivity rate was 58.78%. Rhinovirus had the highest positivity rate (20.95%), followed by respiratory syncytial virus (11.12%) and Mycoplasma pneumoniae (6.89%). Co-infections were identified in 8.08% of specimens. Multivariable logistic regression revealed distinct age, sex, and seasonal patterns for the three most common pathogens: M. pneumoniae risk increased progressively with age (≥ 6 years: OR = 6.100), RSV predominated in infants, and rhinovirus showed a less pronounced age pattern. Across four pandemic phases (pre-pandemic, strict NPIs, transition, and post-restriction), pathogen detection exhibited distinct trajectories. pneumoniae dropped sharply during the pandemic period and resurged post-restriction (from 1.11% to 12.61%); rhinovirus declined during strict NPIs but rebounded quickly and remained stable; RSV paradoxically increased during strict NPIs (from 10.66% to 26.74%). This study provides evidence on pediatric respiratory pathogen epidemiology in Fuzhou, revealing pathogen-specific changes in detection patterns across different pandemic phases. These findings underscore the importance of continued surveillance and pathogen-targeted prevention strategies in the post-pandemic era.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the need to understand how the COVID-19 pandemic altered the epidemiology of pediatric respiratory pathogens in Fuzhou, focusing on pathogen-specific changes and gaps in knowledge about their distribution patterns in children.
Design and population
This retrospective study enrolled 37,724 children aged 0–18 years hospitalized for acute respiratory infections at Fuzhou Children’s Hospital between June 2019 and May 2024, including 22,327 males and 15,397 females.
Core methods
Nasal swab specimens were tested for 11 respiratory pathogens using multiplex PCR, and multivariable logistic regression was applied to analyze age, sex, and seasonal patterns for the most common pathogens.
Main results
RSV positivity was 11.12% overall, with multivariable analysis showing RSV predominance in infants; RSV detection paradoxically increased during strict non-pharmaceutical interventions (from 10.66% to 26.74%) and declined post-restriction.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted RSV’s atypical rise during strict NPIs as a potential consequence of reduced early-life exposure leading to delayed infections, highlighting pathogen-specific pandemic effects not previously detailed in Fuzhou.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support targeted RSV surveillance and prevention strategies in the post-pandemic era, particularly for infants who remain at highest risk during atypical circulation periods.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s single-center retrospective design and reliance on hospital-based sampling limit generalizability to community settings, warranting cautious interpretation of RSV-specific trends.

用于季节性传染病干预措施因果评估的贝叶斯流行病对齐

Bayesian epidemic alignment for causal evaluation of seasonal infectious-disease interventions
作者： David Moriña
摘要中文翻译季节性传染病干预措施通常采用中断时间序列或按日历周调整流行病的前后设计进行评估。当不同季节流行病的发作、速度或高峰时间不同时,这种比较就会混淆流行病阶段的转变与疾病负担的变化。我们提出了一种贝叶斯因果计数模型,其中特定季节的仿射变换将日历时间映射到潜在的流行病时钟,并根据该时钟而不是日历来估计干预效果。对齐是一个模型组件而不是预处理步骤,因此流行时间的不确定性会传播到每个因果对比中。该模型使用负二项式观测分布、分层区域、季节和区域-季节效应、收缩的傅立叶流行曲线和连续的程序强度暴露。在受控对比度和传播每只手臂内疾病史的动态对比度下,后 g 计算可预防病例、预防分数、峰值衰减和流行性位移。两层模拟研究评估稳定时间、流行病时钟变化、强度相关确定和区域级混杂下的偏差、均方根误差、区间覆盖和参数恢复。我们使用开放的加泰罗尼亚初级保健 监测 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 免疫数据来说明该框架,并明确注意识别效果的计划强度的重叠。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
季节性传染病干预措施通常采用中断时间序列或按日历周调整流行病的前后设计进行评估。该研究通过提出贝叶斯因果计数模型来解决这些方法的局限性,该模型可以解释流行时间的不确定性。
设计与对象
该研究提出了贝叶斯因果计数模型来评估季节性传染病干预措施。它使用开放的加泰罗尼亚初级保健 监测 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 免疫数据来说明该框架。
核心方法
该模型使用负二项式观测分布、分层区域、季节和区域-季节效应、收缩的傅立叶流行曲线和连续的程序强度暴露。在受控对比度和传播每只手臂内疾病史的动态对比度下,后 g 计算可预防病例、预防分数、峰值衰减和流行性位移。
主要结果
对齐是一个模型组件而不是预处理步骤,因此流行时间的不确定性会传播到每个因果对比中。
解释与创新
该模型通过将流行时间的不确定性直接纳入分析框架,为季节性传染病干预措施的因果评估提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
该框架可以为设计更准确的 监测 系统和 呼吸道合胞病毒 干预措施提供信息,特别是在计划强度与流行病时间重叠的情况下。
局限与证据强度
两层模拟研究评估稳定时间、流行病时钟变化、强度相关确定和区域级混杂下的偏差、均方根误差、区间覆盖和参数恢复。由于该研究基于模拟和仅抽象数据,因此证据并不充分。

Bayesian epidemic alignment for causal evaluation of seasonal infectious-disease interventions

Authors: David Moriña
Original abstract (English)
Seasonal infectious-disease interventions are commonly evaluated with interrupted time-series or pre--post designs that align epidemics by calendar week. When epidemic onset, speed or peak timing differs between seasons, such comparisons confound a shift in epidemic phase with a change in disease burden. We propose a Bayesian causal count model in which season-specific affine transformations map calendar time to a latent epidemic clock, and intervention effects are estimated on that clock rather than on the calendar. The alignment is a model component rather than a preprocessing step, so uncertainty about epidemic timing propagates into every causal contrast. The model uses a negative-binomial observation distribution, hierarchical area, season and area-season effects, a shrunk Fourier epidemic curve, and a continuous programme-intensity exposure. Posterior g-computation yields prevented cases, prevented fractions, peak attenuation and epidemic displacement, under both a controlled contrast and a dynamic contrast that propagates disease history within each arm. A two-tier simulation study evaluates bias, root mean squared error, interval coverage and parameter recovery under stable timing, epidemic-clock variation, intensity-dependent ascertainment and area-level confounding. We illustrate the framework using open Catalan primary-care surveillance and respiratory syncytial virus immunisation data, with explicit attention to the overlap in programme intensity that identifies the effect.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Seasonal infectious-disease interventions are commonly evaluated with interrupted time-series or pre-post designs that align epidemics by calendar week. The study addresses the limitations of these methods by proposing a Bayesian causal count model that accounts for uncertainty in epidemic timing.
Design and population
The study proposes a Bayesian causal count model to evaluate seasonal infectious-disease interventions. It uses open Catalan primary-care surveillance and respiratory syncytial virus immunisation data to illustrate the framework.
Core methods
The model uses a negative-binomial observation distribution, hierarchical area, season and area-season effects, a shrunk Fourier epidemic curve, and a continuous programme-intensity exposure. Posterior g-computation yields prevented cases, prevented fractions, peak attenuation and epidemic displacement, under both a controlled contrast and a dynamic contrast that propagates disease history within each arm.
Main results
The alignment is a model component rather than a preprocessing step, so uncertainty about epidemic timing propagates into every causal contrast.
Interpretation and novelty
The model provides a novel approach to causal evaluation of seasonal infectious-disease interventions by incorporating uncertainty in epidemic timing directly into the analysis framework.
Scientific and public-health significance
The framework can inform the design of more accurate surveillance systems and interventions for respiratory syncytial virus, particularly in contexts where programme intensity overlaps with epidemic timing.
Limitations and evidence strength
A two-tier simulation study evaluates bias, root mean squared error, interval coverage and parameter recovery under stable timing, epidemic-clock variation, intensity-dependent ascertainment and area-level confounding. The evidence is moderate as the study is based on simulation and abstract-only data.

呼吸道合胞病毒 婴儿保护的新时代:当前免疫预防工具的简要回顾

The New Era of Respiratory Syncytial Virus Protection for Infants: A Mini Review of Current Tools for Immunoprophylaxis
作者： Santtu Heinonen, Pekka Vartiainen
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的 这篇简短的综述介绍了婴儿 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 免疫预防的历史以及该领域的最新进展。它还回顾了最近引入的预防策略的有效性、安全性和影响。方法 本叙述性综述基于对已发表文献的有针对性的综述,重点是关键临床试验、监管文件和最新的现实世界证据。结果 自 2022 年以来,已有三种用于预防婴儿 RSV 的免疫产品获得批准。它们是两种长效单克隆抗体 尼塞韦单抗 和 clesrovimab,以及一种母体 RSV 疫苗。这些干预措施对就诊的 RSV 相关下呼吸道感染的疗效为 49% 至 76%,对因 RSV 相关下呼吸道感染而住院的疗效为 55% 至 91%。不同产品和随访时间的疗效各不相同。在获得许可后的真实世界证据研究中也报告了类似或更高的有效性。总体而言,这些干预措施的耐受性良好,且安全性良好。结论 新推出的长效单克隆抗体和母源RSV 疫苗已被证明具有高效性且安全性令人放心。这些措施已在许多国家实施,并大大减少了婴儿的呼吸道合胞病毒负担。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述研究了人类 呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV;人正肺病毒,包括 RSV-A 和 RSV-B)对婴儿的免疫预防,重点关注最近引入的预防策略的有效性、安全性和影响的核心问题。
证据基础与方法
这篇叙述性综述综合了已发表的文献,重点是关键的临床试验、监管文件和最近的现实世界证据;没有明确报告资格标准和选择方法。
共识与结论
自 2022 年以来,已有三种婴儿免疫预防产品获得批准:两种长效单克隆抗体(尼塞韦单抗 和 clesrovimab)和一种母体 RSV 疫苗,对就诊的 RSV 相关下呼吸道感染的疗效为 49% 至 76%,对因 RSV 相关下呼吸道感染而住院的疗效为 55% 至 91%。
争议与缺口
该审查没有报告按地理区域、社会经济状况或高风险亚组划分的疗效估计的异质性,也没有解决初始批准阶段之后的长期安全性。
科研与实践启示
许可后有效性研究应优先监测保护的持久性、早产儿的安全性以及资源匮乏环境中的实施策略,以优化全球 RSV 负担减轻。

The New Era of Respiratory Syncytial Virus Protection for Infants: A Mini Review of Current Tools for Immunoprophylaxis

Authors: Santtu Heinonen, Pekka Vartiainen
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Aim This mini review describes the history of infant respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) immunoprophylaxis and the latest advances in this area. It also reviews the efficacy, safety and impact of the recently introduced prophylactic strategies. Method This narrative review is based on a targeted review of published literature, with emphasis on pivotal clinical trials, regulatory documents and recent real‐world evidence. Results Three immunisation products for preventing RSV in infants have been approved since 2022. These are two long‐acting monoclonal antibodies, nirsevimab and clesrovimab, and a maternal RSV vaccine. The efficacy of these interventions has ranged from 49% to 76% against medically attended RSV‐associated lower respiratory tract infections and from 55% to 91% against hospitalisation due to RSV‐associated lower respiratory tract infection. The efficacy varied between the products and the duration of the follow‐up. Similar or higher effectiveness has been reported in post‐licensure, real‐world evidence studies. Overall, these interventions have been well tolerated, with favourable safety profiles. Conclusion The newly introduced long‐acting monoclonal antibodies and the maternal RSV vaccine have proved highly effective and have demonstrated reassuring safety. They have already been implemented in a number of countries and have substantially reduced the RSV burden in infants.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human respiratory syncytial virus (RSV; Orthopneumovirus hominis, including RSV-A and RSV-B) immunoprophylaxis for infants, focusing on the central question of the efficacy, safety, and impact of recently introduced prophylactic strategies.
Evidence base and method
This narrative review synthesizes published literature with emphasis on pivotal clinical trials, regulatory documents, and recent real-world evidence; eligibility criteria and selection methods are not explicitly reported.
Consensus and conclusions
Three immunoprophylaxis products for infants have been approved since 2022: two long-acting monoclonal antibodies (nirsevimab and clesrovimab) and a maternal RSV vaccine, with efficacy ranging from 49% to 76% against medically attended RSV-associated lower respiratory tract infections and 55% to 91% against hospitalization due to RSV-associated lower respiratory tract infection.
Controversies and gaps
The review does not report heterogeneity in efficacy estimates by geographic region, socioeconomic status, or high-risk subgroups, nor does it address long-term safety beyond initial approval phases.
Research and practice implications
Post-licensure effectiveness studies should prioritize monitoring durability of protection, safety in preterm infants, and implementation strategies in low-resource settings to optimize global RSV burden reduction.

医疗机构中的 呼吸道合胞病毒 暴发:文献综述。

Respiratory syncytial virus outbreaks in healthcare facilities: A review of the literature.
作者： Ilektra Tseliou, Helena C Maltezou
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是呼吸道感染的主要原因。暴发 已发生在各种医疗保健环境中。我们审查了医疗机构中的 RSV 暴发。2010 年至 2025 年期间,PubMed 上搜索了有关医疗机构中 RSV 暴发 的文章。审查了 18 篇介绍 19 RSV 暴发 的文章。11 起 暴发 发生在血液科/肿瘤科/移植病房/病房,6 起发生在新生儿重症监护病房 (NICU),2 起发生在老年医院/长期护理机构 (LTCF)。所有 暴发 的平均发作率为 26.04%(范围:12.00%-68.96%),平均持续时间为 39.9(范围:5-131)天。每个 暴发 平均有 20.31 名(范围:4-63)名患者受到影响。无症状或症状轻微的医护人员(HCP)是最常见的感染源。RSV 疾病在早产儿和免疫抑制患者中尤为严重。早产儿和免疫抑制患者中也发生了长时间的病毒脱落,这对 暴发 的遏制提出了挑战。RSV 感染患者的总体病死率 (CFR) 为 11.64%(平均 CFR:血液科/肿瘤科/移植科室为 13.99%,NICU 为 6.49%,老年科诊所为 6.25%)。当帕利珠单抗预防作为多模式干预的一部分时,与 暴发 控制暂时相关。总之,严重的 RSV 暴发 发生在照顾弱势患者的医疗机构中。此类 暴发 经常被追溯到 HCP,通常持续数周,并影响许多患者,这强调了医疗机构内 RSV 的 传播 动态,但也暴露了感染控制方面的差距。老年患者、长期护理中心的居民和免疫功能低下的患者应接种 RSV 疫苗。应研究帕利珠单抗的预防作用。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述检查了医疗机构中的人类 呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV;人正肺病毒,包括 RSV-A 和 RSV-B)暴发,重点关注早产儿、免疫抑制患者和长期护理机构老年居民等弱势患者群体。核心问题解决了这些设置中 RSV 暴发 的 传播 动态、严重性和控制。
证据基础与方法
这是一篇叙述性综述,在 2010 年至 2025 年期间在 PubMed 中搜索了有关医疗机构中 RSV 暴发 的文章。纳入了 19 篇 暴发 的 18 篇文章,涵盖血液科/肿瘤科/移植病房 (11 暴发)、新生儿重症监护病房 (6 暴发) 和老年医院/长期护理机构 (2 暴发)。
共识与结论
RSV 暴发 在医疗机构中很常见,通常可追溯到无症状或症状轻微的医护人员,通常会持续数周。暴发 的平均攻击率为 26.04%(范围:12.00%-68.96%),平均持续时间为 39.9 天(范围:5-131 天)。感染患者的总体病死率为11.64%,其中血液科/肿瘤科/移植科室的病死率(13.99%)高于新生儿重症监护病房(6.49%)和老年诊所(6.25%)。
争议与缺口
早产儿和免疫抑制患者的病毒长时间脱落对 暴发 的遏制提出了挑战,该审查强调了感染控制实践中的差距。特定高风险环境中的证据基础仅限于 19 个 暴发,这使得其他医疗环境和人群中的 传播 动态存在不确定性。
科研与实践启示
优先研究应调查无症状医护人员在 RSV 传播 中的作用,并评估多模式干预措施,包括帕利珠单抗预防,以控制 暴发。高危病房需要加强 监测 和感染控制措施,特别是对于老年和免疫功能低下的患者,以降低 暴发 的严重程度和 死亡率。

Respiratory syncytial virus outbreaks in healthcare facilities: A review of the literature.

Authors: Ilektra Tseliou, Helena C Maltezou
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of respiratory infections. Outbreaks have occurred in various healthcare settings. We reviewed RSV outbreaks in healthcare facilities. PubMed was searched from 2010 to 2025 for articles on RSV outbreaks in healthcare facilities. Eighteen articles presenting 19 RSV outbreaks were reviewed. Eleven outbreaks occurred in hematology/oncology/transplantation wards/units, six in neonatal intensive care units (NICUs), and two in geriatric hospitals/long-term care facilities (LTCFs). The mean attack rate across all outbreaks was 26.04% (range: 12.00%-68.96%) and the mean duration was 39.9 (range: 5-131) days. A mean of 20.31 (range: 4-63) patients were affected per outbreak. Asymptomatic or mildly symptomatic healthcare personnel (HCP) were the most frequent sources of infection. RSV disease was particularly severe in premature neonates and immunosuppressed patients. Prolonged virus shedding also occurred among premature neonates and immunosuppressed patients, challenging the containment of outbreaks. The overall case fatality rate (CFR) among RSV-infected patients was 11.64% (mean CFR: 13.99% in hematology/oncology/transplantation units, 6.49% in NICUs, and 6.25% in geriatric clinics). Palivizumab prophylaxis was temporally associated with outbreak control when used as part of multimodal interventions. In conclusion, serious RSV outbreaks occur in healthcare facilities caring for vulnerable patients. Such outbreaks are frequently traced to HCP, usually last several weeks, and affect many patients, which emphasize the transmission dynamics of RSV within healthcare facilities but also disclose gaps in infection control. Elderly patients, residents of LTCFs, and immunocompromised patients should be vaccinated against RSV. Palivizumab prophylaxis should be studied.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human respiratory syncytial virus (RSV; Orthopneumovirus hominis, including RSV-A and RSV-B) outbreaks in healthcare facilities, focusing on vulnerable patient populations such as premature neonates, immunosuppressed patients, and elderly residents of long-term care facilities. The central question addresses the transmission dynamics, severity, and control of RSV outbreaks within these settings.
Evidence base and method
This is a narrative review that searched PubMed from 2010 to 2025 for articles on RSV outbreaks in healthcare facilities. Eighteen articles presenting 19 outbreaks were included, covering hematology/oncology/transplantation wards (11 outbreaks), neonatal intensive care units (6 outbreaks), and geriatric hospitals/long-term care facilities (2 outbreaks).
Consensus and conclusions
RSV outbreaks in healthcare facilities are frequent, often traced to asymptomatic or mildly symptomatic healthcare personnel, and typically last several weeks. The mean attack rate across outbreaks was 26.04% (range: 12.00%-68.96%), with a mean duration of 39.9 days (range: 5-131 days). The overall case fatality rate among infected patients was 11.64%, with higher rates in hematology/oncology/transplantation units (13.99%) compared to neonatal intensive care units (6.49%) and geriatric clinics (6.25%).
Controversies and gaps
Prolonged virus shedding in premature neonates and immunosuppressed patients challenges outbreak containment, and the review highlights gaps in infection control practices. The evidence base is limited to 19 outbreaks across specific high-risk settings, leaving uncertainty about transmission dynamics in other healthcare environments and populations.
Research and practice implications
Priority research should investigate the role of asymptomatic healthcare personnel in RSV transmission and evaluate multimodal interventions, including palivizumab prophylaxis, for outbreak control. Enhanced surveillance and infection control measures are needed in high-risk units, particularly for elderly and immunocompromised patients, to reduce outbreak severity and mortality.

Journal: Expert Review of Vaccines  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-14  |  DOI: 10.1080/14760584.2026.2715855  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 25, 1, 2715855
C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

基于共识的专家关于新兴市场国家老年人 RSV 感染管理和预防的建议:专家小组结构化调查的结果

Consensus based expert recommendations for management and prevention of RSV infections in older adults in emerging market countries: results from an expert panel structured survey
作者： Otavio Cintra, Zainab Alsharef, Abhay Phansalkar, Antonio Anzueto, Mark Loeb
摘要中文翻译背景:呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 可导致老年人 (OA) 患严重疾病,尤其是患有合并症的老年人。它是 60 岁以上成人急性呼吸道疾病和下呼吸道感染的主要原因。它对发病率、死亡率 和医疗保健系统的影响正变得越来越明显。由于诊断不足以及与其他呼吸道疾病的相似性,人们对疾病负担 (BoD) 提出了疑问。第一次预防机会出现在 2023 年和 2024 年,疫苗 获得批准的年龄≥60 岁,将资格扩大到其他年龄组。研究设计和方法:采用改良的德尔菲法,通过问卷调查和来自 12 个新兴市场国家的 27 名呼吸道和传染病专家参加的虚拟共识研讨会,就 OA 中 RSV 的 诊断、预防和 治疗 达成共识。结果:专家组建议为所有年龄≥70岁的成年人引入通用的疫苗接种计划,并为所有年龄≥60岁有合并症的成年人引入疫苗接种。还同意需要提高认识、更好的测试以及国家和国际指南。结论:协议强调应优先考虑用于 OA 的 RSV 疫苗接种。对临床医生和患者进行教育、改进 监测 并将 RSV 疫苗接种 纳入卫生政策是减少 OA 中 BoD 的关键。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该范围检查了老年人 (OA),特别是年龄≥60 岁的成年人中的人类 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV),重点关注其作为该人群急性呼吸道疾病和下呼吸道感染的主要原因的作用。
证据基础与方法
该审查采用改进的德尔菲法,通过问卷调查和来自12个新兴市场国家的27名专家参加的虚拟共识研讨会,就OA中RSV的诊断、预防和治疗达成共识。
共识与结论
专家组共识建议对所有年龄≥70岁的成年人通用疫苗接种,对所有年龄≥60岁有合并症的成年人推荐疫苗接种,强调优先考虑RSV 疫苗接种用于OA。
争议与缺口
该综述强调,诊断不足以及与其他呼吸道疾病的症状重叠是准确评估 OA 中 RSV 引起的疾病负担 (BoD) 的关键挑战。
科研与实践启示
主要优先事项包括教育临床医生和患者、改进 监测 系统以及将 RSV 疫苗接种 纳入国家和国际卫生政策,以减少 OA 中的 BoD。

Consensus based expert recommendations for management and prevention of RSV infections in older adults in emerging market countries: results from an expert panel structured survey

Authors: Otavio Cintra, Zainab Alsharef, Abhay Phansalkar, Antonio Anzueto, Mark Loeb
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) can cause serious disease in older adults (OAs), particularly those with comorbidities. It is a leading cause of acute respiratory illness and lower respiratory tract infections in adults ≥60 years. Its impact on morbidity, mortality, and healthcare systems is becoming evident. Questions about the burden of disease (BoD) are raised due to underdiagnosis and the similarity with other respiratory illnesses. First opportunities for prevention came in 2023 and 2024, with vaccines approved for ≥60 years, expanding eligibility to additional age groups. RESEARCH DESIGN AND METHODS: A modified Delphi approach through a questionnaire survey and virtual consensus workshop with 27 experts in respiratory and infectious diseases from 12 emerging market countries was done to reach consensus on diagnosis, prevention, and treatment of RSV in OAs. RESULTS: The panel recommends the introduction of a universal vaccination program for all adults aged ≥70 years, and vaccination for all adults aged ≥60 years with comorbidities. The need for increased awareness, better testing, and national and international guidelines was also agreed. CONCLUSION: The agreements highlight that RSV vaccination for OAs should be prioritized. Educating clinicians and patients, improving surveillance, and integrating RSV vaccination into health policies are key to reducing BoD in OAs.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The scope examines human respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in older adults (OAs), specifically adults aged ≥60 years, focusing on its role as a leading cause of acute respiratory illness and lower respiratory tract infections in this population.
Evidence base and method
The review employs a modified Delphi approach through a questionnaire survey and virtual consensus workshop involving 27 experts from 12 emerging market countries to reach consensus on diagnosis, prevention, and treatment of RSV in OAs.
Consensus and conclusions
The expert panel consensus recommends universal vaccination for all adults aged ≥70 years and vaccination for all adults aged ≥60 years with comorbidities, emphasizing the prioritization of RSV vaccination for OAs.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights underdiagnosis and symptom overlap with other respiratory illnesses as key challenges in accurately assessing the burden of disease (BoD) caused by RSV in OAs.
Research and practice implications
Key priorities include educating clinicians and patients, improving surveillance systems, and integrating RSV vaccination into national and international health policies to reduce BoD in OAs.

网络驱动的 抗病毒药物 新兴 RNA 病毒发现管道:来自 Red Mexicana de Desarrollo de 抗病毒药物 (RMDA) 的关键视角。

A network-driven antiviral discovery pipeline for emerging RNA viruses: a critical perspective from the Red Mexicana de Desarrollo de Antivirales (RMDA).
作者： Luis Adrián De Jesús-González, Alan Orlando Santos-Mena, Carlos Noé Farfán-Morales, Moisés León-Juárez, José Manuel Reyes-Ruiz, Juan Fidel Osuna-Ramos, Carlos Daniel Cordero-Rivera, Selvin Noé Palacios-Rápalo, Flor Itzel Lira-Hernández, Rosa María Del Ángel

A network-driven antiviral discovery pipeline for emerging RNA viruses: a critical perspective from the Red Mexicana de Desarrollo de Antivirales (RMDA).

Authors: Luis Adrián De Jesús-González, Alan Orlando Santos-Mena, Carlos Noé Farfán-Morales, Moisés León-Juárez, José Manuel Reyes-Ruiz, Juan Fidel Osuna-Ramos, Carlos Daniel Cordero-Rivera, Selvin Noé Palacios-Rápalo, Flor Itzel Lira-Hernández, Rosa María Del Ángel
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: RNA viruses impose a disproportionate burden of emerging and reemerging infectious diseases in Latin America, yet antiviral discovery in the region remains fragmented, target-limited, and frequently disconnected from translational pathways. This perspective discusses the Red Mexicana de Desarrollo de Antivirales (RMDA) as a network-driven model to organize antiviral discovery against clinically relevant RNA viruses. AREAS COVERED: The authors describe the conceptual architecture of RMDA, a multi-institutional Mexican initiative that integrates computational prioritization, cell-based antiviral evaluation under BSL-2/BSL-3 conditions, mechanistic virology, assessment of pharmacological synergy, in vivo validation, and clinical linkage through healthcare systems such as the Instituto Mexicano del Seguro Social. The framework is supported by prior work from participating groups on host-directed and virus-directed candidates, including metabolic modulators, lipid-lowering agents, nuclear transport inhibitors, natural products, polyphenols, melatonin, and rational drug combinations evaluated against dengue virus, Zika virus, SARS-CoV-2, respiratory syncytial virus, and related RNA viruses. EXPERT OPINION: RMDA provides a scalable and regionally adaptable model for antiviral drug discovery in low- and middle-income settings. By aligning distributed expertise, standardized workflows, and translational decision points, this framework may accelerate candidate prioritization, reduce duplication, and strengthen preparedness against RNA virus threats in Mexico and Latin America.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
AREAS COVERED: The authors describe the conceptual architecture of RMDA, a multi-institutional Mexican initiative that integrates computational prioritization, cell-based antiviral evaluation under BSL-2/BSL-3 conditions, mechanistic virology, assessment of pharmacological synergy, in vivo validation, and clinical linkage through healthcare systems such as the Instituto Mexicano del Seguro Social.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review method, databases, eligibility process, or evidence base.
Consensus and conclusions
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a stable consensus or central conclusion.
Controversies and gaps
RNA viruses impose a disproportionate burden of emerging and reemerging infectious diseases in Latin America, yet antiviral discovery in the region remains fragmented, target-limited, and frequently disconnected from translational pathways.
Research and practice implications
By aligning distributed expertise, standardized workflows, and translational decision points, this framework may accelerate candidate prioritization, reduce duplication, and strengthen preparedness against RNA virus threats in Mexico and Latin America.

宿主-病毒相互作用的表观转录组调控。

Epitranscriptomic regulation of host-virus interactions.
作者： Simran Mehra, Sushant Phadnis, Y S Ravi Kumar, Anjali Singh, Bhupendra Verma
摘要中文翻译RNA 化学修饰统称为表观转录组,现在被认为是影响 宿主 抗病毒药物 反应的基因表达的关键调节因子。RNA 和 DNA 病毒经常利用这些修饰来增强其复制并逃避免疫检测。这篇综述探讨了关键的 RNA 修饰、主要修饰酶,强调这些修饰如何调节 宿主 和病毒 RNA 的 mRNA 稳定性、翻译和先天免疫识别。此外,本综述还研究了病毒(包括 DENV、SARS-CoV-2、HIV-1、HCV、RSV 和 HSV)如何操纵 宿主 RNA 修饰系统。总的来说,这篇综述强调了表观转录组作为 宿主-病毒相互作用中的动态界面,并强调了其作为 抗病毒药物 治疗开发目标的潜力。

Epitranscriptomic regulation of host-virus interactions.

Authors: Simran Mehra, Sushant Phadnis, Y S Ravi Kumar, Anjali Singh, Bhupendra Verma
Original abstract (English)
RNA chemical modifications, collectively termed the epitranscriptome, are now recognized as critical regulators of gene expression that influence host antiviral responses. These modifications are frequently co-opted by both RNA and DNA viruses to enhance their replication and evade immune detection. This review explores key RNA modifications, principal modifying enzymes, emphasizing how these modifications regulate mRNA stability, translation, and innate immune recognition of both host and viral RNAs. Furthermore, this review examines how viruses, including DENV, SARS-CoV-2, HIV-1, HCV, RSV, and HSV, manipulate host RNA modification systems. Overall, this review highlights the epitranscriptome as a dynamic interface in host-virus interactions and underscores its potential as a target for antiviral therapeutic development.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review explores key RNA modifications, principal modifying enzymes, emphasizing how these modifications regulate mRNA stability, translation, and innate immune recognition of both host and viral RNAs.
Evidence base and method
The review provides a general overview of epitranscriptomic regulation of host-virus interactions, focusing on RNA chemical modifications and their role in antiviral responses. It does not report specific databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, or meta-analysis methods.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights the epitranscriptome as a dynamic interface in host-virus interactions and underscores its potential as a target for antiviral therapeutic development. It emphasizes the role of RNA modifications in regulating mRNA stability, translation, and immune recognition.
Controversies and gaps
The review does not report specific controversies or evidence gaps related to the epitranscriptomic regulation of host-virus interactions. It focuses on general observations and does not address unresolved mechanisms or conflicting findings.
Research and practice implications
The review underscores the potential of the epitranscriptome as a target for antiviral therapeutic development, suggesting future research priorities in understanding how viruses manipulate host RNA modification systems.

Deadbolt 药物发现:通过小分子锁定 1 类病毒进入之门。

Deadbolt Drug Discovery: Locking the Door to Class 1 Viral Entry by Small Molecules.
作者： John P Sloan, Lijun Rong, Terry W Moore
摘要中文翻译病毒病原体的持续出现强调了对具有改进功效的新型 抗病毒药物 疗法的需求。病毒进入的小分子抑制剂是有吸引力的 抗病毒药物 药物,因为它们可以在病毒的最早阶段阻断病毒,防止病毒进入 宿主 细胞并造成下游损伤。一些对人类健康最关心的病毒,如人类免疫缺陷病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒、流感病毒、埃博拉病毒、严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2和拉沙病毒,编码介导膜融合和病毒进入的1类融合蛋白。在这篇综述中,我们重点介绍了过去五年中报道的针对编码 1 类融合蛋白的病毒的小分子进入抑制剂的发现和开发的最新进展。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是开发针对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 中 1 类融合蛋白的小分子抑制剂,以防止病毒进入 宿主 细胞。核心问题是这些抑制剂是否可以作为 RSV 相关疾病的有效 抗病毒药物 疗法。
证据基础与方法
该综述对过去五年来针对具有 1 类融合蛋白(包括 RSV)的病毒的小分子进入抑制剂的最新进展进行了叙述性综合。它不报告特定的数据库、搜索日期或荟萃分析方法。
共识与结论
该综述强调,针对 1 类融合蛋白的小分子抑制剂在防止病毒进入方面显示出前景,这是感染过程中关键的早期步骤。这些抑制剂被认为是有吸引力的 抗病毒药物 药物,因为它们具有阻止下游损伤的潜力。
争议与缺口
该评论指出,尽管人们对小分子进入抑制剂的兴趣日益浓厚,但证据仍然有限且异质。目前缺乏关于其在不同 RSV 毒株和群体中的功效的全面数据,需要进一步研究来解决这些差距。
科研与实践启示
该综述建议未来的研究应优先开发和测试针对 RSV 中 1 类融合蛋白的小分子抑制剂。这包括评估它们对 RSV-A 和 RSV-B 菌株的有效性,并探索它们在更广泛的 抗病毒药物 应用中的潜力。

Deadbolt Drug Discovery: Locking the Door to Class 1 Viral Entry by Small Molecules.

Authors: John P Sloan, Lijun Rong, Terry W Moore
Original abstract (English)
The continued emergence of viral pathogens underscores the need for novel antiviral therapeutics with improved efficacy. Small-molecule inhibitors of viral entry are attractive antiviral agents because they block the virus at its earliest step, preventing the virus from entering host cells and causing downstream damage. Some of the viruses of greatest concern for human health, such as human immunodeficiency virus, respiratory syncytial virus, influenza virus, Ebola virus, severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2, and Lassa virus, encode Class 1 fusion proteins which mediate membrane fusion and viral entry. In this review, we highlight recent advances in the discovery and development of small-molecule entry inhibitors reported over the past five years that target viruses encoding Class 1 fusion proteins.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the development of small-molecule inhibitors targeting Class 1 fusion proteins in respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) to prevent viral entry into host cells. The central question is whether these inhibitors can serve as effective antiviral therapeutics for RSV-related diseases.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of recent advances in small-molecule entry inhibitors targeting viruses with Class 1 fusion proteins, including RSV, over the past five years. It does not report specific databases, search dates, or meta-analysis methods.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that small-molecule inhibitors targeting Class 1 fusion proteins have shown promise in preventing viral entry, which is a critical early step in the infection process. These inhibitors are considered attractive antiviral agents due to their potential to block downstream damage.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that while there is growing interest in small-molecule entry inhibitors, the evidence is still limited and heterogeneous. There is a lack of comprehensive data on their efficacy across different RSV strains and populations, and further research is needed to address these gaps.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that future research should prioritize the development and testing of small-molecule inhibitors targeting Class 1 fusion proteins in RSV. This includes evaluating their effectiveness against RSV-A and RSV-B strains and exploring their potential for broader antiviral applications.

潮汐呼吸分析:一种潜在的非侵入性床旁方法,用于客观评估急性 毛细支气管炎—病例报告 的呼吸力学和支气管扩张剂反应性以及文献回顾

Tidal-Breathing Analysis: A Potential Non-Invasive, Bedside Method for Objective Assessment of Respiratory Mechanics and Bronchodilator Responsiveness in Acute Bronchiolitis—Case Report and Review of the Literature
作者： Mihai Craiu, Radu Marian Gheorghiu, Alexandra Bratu, Iustina-Violeta Stan
摘要中文翻译背景:急性 毛细支气管炎 是婴儿下呼吸道感染的最常见原因之一,并且仍然是一种临床异质综合征。目前的国际指南建议支持性治疗,通常不鼓励常规使用支气管扩张剂;然而,一部分患者可能有生理反应。潮汐呼吸分析 (TBA) 是一种非侵入性床边方法,用于客观评估呼吸力学,并且可以客观量化年龄较大儿童服用支气管扩张剂后的生理变化。病例介绍:我们报告了一名 23 个月大男孩的病例,该男孩患有严重的 RSV 毛细支气管炎 样喘息疾病,并发急性呼吸衰竭。在吸入支气管扩张剂给药前后,使用预定义的 TBA 参数评估支气管扩张剂反应性。治疗 后,患者达到潮气呼气流量峰值的时间 (tPTEF) 从 0.17 秒增加到 0.33 秒 (+94%),而他的 tPTEF/TE 比率从 0.12 增加到 0.27 (+125%)。呼气时间 (TE) 和总呼吸周期持续时间 (Ttot) 分别减少 12.6% 和 5.1%,而潮气量 (VT) 和每分钟通气量 (MV) 增加 12.1% 和 18.3%。呼气峰流量保持不变(支气管扩张剂给药前后均为 0.14 L/s)。讨论:据我们所知,这是使用床边 TBA 来表征严重 毛细支气管炎 类疾病的急性支气管扩张剂反应性的首批报告之一。呼气正时指数的改善,以及呼气峰值流量不变的情况下呼气时间的缩短,与支气管扩张剂给药后呼气流量动力学的改善一致。这些发现可以支持可能存在支气管扩张剂反应性生理表型的假设。先前的研究已证明婴儿期严重的 毛细支气管炎 与随后的复发性喘息或哮喘之间存在关联;然而,通过 TBA 客观测量的急性支气管扩张剂反应性是否表明这种表型代表了未来纵向研究的可检验假设。结论:该病例说明了床边 TBA 确定严重 毛细支气管炎 样疾病中生理性支气管扩张剂反应性的可行性。应进行前瞻性纵向研究,以确定 TBA 定义的反应性是否代表需要前瞻性验证的早期生理标志,以及这种生理分层是否可以支持个体化随访策略。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述检查了婴儿中的人类 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 毛细支气管炎,重点关注潮汐呼吸分析 (TBA) 是否可以客观评估 RSV 引起的急性 毛细支气管炎 样疾病的呼吸力学和支气管扩张剂反应性的核心问题。
证据基础与方法
该评论是 病例报告 和叙事综合,没有进行系统搜索或荟萃分析;它描述了一名患有严重 RSV 毛细支气管炎 样疾病的患者,并在吸入支气管扩张剂给药前后使用预定义的 TBA 参数评估支气管扩张剂反应性。
共识与结论
该综述得出的结论是,TBA 可用于识别严重 毛细支气管炎 类疾病中的生理支气管扩张剂反应,观察到潮气呼气流量峰值时间 (tPTEF) 和 tPTEF/TE 比值增加,同时呼气时间和总呼吸周期持续时间减少,潮气量和每分钟通气量增加。
争议与缺口
该综述强调了 TBA 定义的支气管扩张剂反应性是否代表随后复发性喘息或哮喘的早期生理标志的不确定性,并指出这一假设需要在纵向研究中进行前瞻性验证。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,需要进行前瞻性纵向研究来确定 TBA 定义的反应性是否可以支持个体化随访策略和早期识别高危婴儿。

Tidal-Breathing Analysis: A Potential Non-Invasive, Bedside Method for Objective Assessment of Respiratory Mechanics and Bronchodilator Responsiveness in Acute Bronchiolitis—Case Report and Review of the Literature

Authors: Mihai Craiu, Radu Marian Gheorghiu, Alexandra Bratu, Iustina-Violeta Stan
Original abstract (English)
Background: Acute bronchiolitis is among the most common causes of lower-respiratory-tract infection in infancy and remains a clinically heterogeneous syndrome. Current international guidelines recommend supportive management and generally discourage routine bronchodilator administration; however, a subset of patients may be physiologically responsive. Tidal-breathing analysis (TBA) is a non-invasive, bedside method for objectively assessing respiratory mechanics and may objectively quantify physiological changes following bronchodilator administration in older children. Case Presentation: We report the case of a 23-month-old boy presenting with a severe RSV bronchiolitis-like wheezing illness complicated by acute respiratory failure. Bronchodilator responsiveness was evaluated using predefined TBA parameters before and after administration of an inhaled bronchodilator. Following treatment, the patient’s time to peak tidal expiratory flow (tPTEF) increased from 0.17 to 0.33 s (+94%), while his tPTEF/TE ratio increased from 0.12 to 0.27 (+125%). Expiratory time (TE) and total respiratory cycle duration (Ttot) decreased by 12.6% and 5.1%, respectively, whereas tidal volume (VT) and minute ventilation (MV) increased by 12.1% and 18.3%. Peak expiratory flow remained unchanged (0.14 L/s before and after bronchodilator administration). Discussion: To the best of our knowledge, this is among the first reports of using bedside TBA to characterize acute bronchodilator responsiveness in a severe bronchiolitis-like illness. The improvement in expiratory timing indices, along with the shorter expiratory time without changes in peak expiratory flow, is consistent with improved expiratory flow dynamics following bronchodilator administration. These findings could support the hypothesis that a bronchodilator-responsive physiological phenotype may exist. Previous studies have demonstrated an association between severe bronchiolitis during infancy and subsequent recurrent wheezing or asthma; however, whether acute bronchodilator responsiveness, measured objectively via TBA, is an indication of this phenotype represents a testable hypothesis for future longitudinal studies. Conclusions: This case illustrates the feasibility of bedside TBA for identifying physiological bronchodilator responsiveness in severe bronchiolitis-like illness. Prospective longitudinal studies should be conducted to determine whether TBA-defined responsiveness represents an early physiological marker requiring prospective validation and whether such physiological stratification may support individualized follow-up strategies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) bronchiolitis in infants, focusing on the central question of whether tidal-breathing analysis (TBA) can objectively assess respiratory mechanics and bronchodilator responsiveness in acute bronchiolitis-like illness caused by RSV.
Evidence base and method
The review is a case report and narrative synthesis without a systematic search or meta-analysis; it describes a single patient with severe RSV bronchiolitis-like illness and evaluates bronchodilator responsiveness using predefined TBA parameters before and after inhaled bronchodilator administration.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that TBA is feasible for identifying physiological bronchodilator responsiveness in severe bronchiolitis-like illness, with observed increases in time to peak tidal expiratory flow (tPTEF) and tPTEF/TE ratio, alongside decreases in expiratory time and total respiratory cycle duration, and increases in tidal volume and minute ventilation.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights uncertainty regarding whether TBA-defined bronchodilator responsiveness represents an early physiological marker of subsequent recurrent wheezing or asthma, noting that this hypothesis requires prospective validation in longitudinal studies.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that prospective longitudinal studies are needed to determine whether TBA-defined responsiveness can support individualized follow-up strategies and early identification of at-risk infants.

呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)

Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV)
新闻简报呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 仍然是一个重大的全球健康威胁,特别是影响婴儿和老年人。婴儿期早期 RSV 感染会增加儿童哮喘的风险,尤其是有过敏或哮喘家族史的儿童。对于老年人来说,RSV 会导致严重的后果和心脏并发症,疫苗 可以有效地将 60 岁及以上成年人的住院率减少 80% 以上。孕产妇 疫苗接种 和 尼塞韦单抗 在新生儿风险最高的月份提供补充保护。世界卫生组织发布了第一份关于婴儿 RSV 免疫的立场文件,强调需要全年获得免疫接种,以尽量减少季节性 暴发。尽管取得了这些进步,但仍需要采取额外的干预措施,因为仅 HEPA 净化器并不能完全减少教室的病毒暴露。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026-08-02。证据中没有报告具体的事件日期。
地点与对象
全球范围,特别关注婴儿、老年人以及早产儿、患有基础疾病的儿童、白血病患者等弱势群体。
事件
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是五岁以下儿童急性下呼吸道感染的主要原因,对全世界儿童健康构成持续威胁。婴儿早期感染 RSV 会显着增加患儿童哮喘的风险,尤其是有过敏或哮喘家族史的儿童。RSV 还会导致老年人出现严重后果和心脏并发症。
规模、影响与风险
RSV 导致成人住院率较高,丹麦的一项大型真实世界随机试验发现,RSVpreF 疫苗 将 60 岁及以上成人与 RSV 相关的住院率降低了 80% 以上。单剂量的 RSV 疫苗 可以为老年人提供 58% 的住院保护,但对于免疫功能低下的个体来说,效果有所不同。全年接种 RSV 疫苗可能会减少全国主要季节性 暴发 的发生。
应对、状态与不确定性
儿科专家正在为呼吸道病毒季节期间 RSV 病例的预期增加做好准备。孕产妇 疫苗接种 和 尼塞韦单抗 共同提供补充策略,在新生儿风险最高的月份提供保护。世界卫生组织 (WHO) 发布了第一份关于保护婴儿免受 RSV 感染的免疫产品的立场文件。然而,HEPA 净化器并不能完全减少教室病毒暴露,这表明需要采取额外的干预措施。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV)

News Brief
Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) remains a significant global health threat, particularly affecting infants and older adults. Early RSV infection in infancy increases the risk of childhood asthma, especially in children with a family history of allergy or asthma. For older adults, RSV causes severe outcomes and heart complications, with vaccines showing effectiveness in reducing hospitalizations by over 80% in adults aged 60 and over. Maternal vaccination and nirsevimab provide complementary protection for newborns during their highest-risk months. The WHO has issued its first position paper on RSV immunization for infants, emphasizing the need for year-round access to immunization to minimize seasonal outbreaks. Despite these advances, additional interventions are needed as HEPA purifiers alone do not fully reduce classroom viral exposure.
Original source text (English)
Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) Nirsevimab is a long-acting monoclonal antibody that provides season-long protection against RSV in infants, reducing medically attended infections and hospitalizations. Maternal vaccination and nirsevimab together offer complementary strategies to protect newborns during their highest-risk months. Time
Published on 2026-08-02. No specific event date is reported in the evidence.
Location and population
Global, with specific focus on infants, older adults, and vulnerable populations such as premature babies, children with underlying diseases, and leukemia patients.
Event
Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) is a leading cause of acute lower respiratory infections in children under five and poses a persistent threat to child health worldwide. Early-infancy infection with RSV significantly increases the risk of developing childhood asthma, especially in children with a family history of allergy or asthma. RSV also causes severe outcomes and heart complications in older adults.
Scale, impact and risk
RSV leads to high hospitalization rates in adults, with a large real-world randomized trial in Denmark finding that the RSVpreF vaccine reduced RSV-related hospitalizations in adults aged 60 and over by over 80%. Year-round access to RSV immunization may reduce major seasonal outbreaks across the nation.

Pediatric experts are preparing for an expected rise in cases of RSV during the respiratory virus season. Maternal vaccination and nirsevimab together offer complementary strategies to protect newborns during their highest-risk months. The World Health Organization (WHO) has published its first-ever position paper on immunization products to protect infants against RSV. However, HEPA purifiers do not fully reduce classroom viral exposure, suggesting additional interventions are needed.

UKHSA 表示,孕产妇 RSV 疫苗 可使新生儿 ICU 入院率减半 - The Pharmaceutical Journal

Maternal RSV vaccine halves newborn ICU admissions, says UKHSA - The Pharmaceutical Journal
新闻简报英国健康安全局 (UKHSA) 报告称,自 2024 年 9 月开始的孕产妇 RSV 疫苗接种 计划使 2025/2026 年冬季期间因 RSV 胸部感染而入院 ICU 和 HDU 的 6 个月以下婴儿减少了 50%,而上一年为 131 例。参与 监测 的 20 个 NHS 信托机构观察到这一显着下降。该报告强调,2025 年 3 月至 2026 年 2 月期间,61.7% 的分娩妇女接受了 RSV 疫苗。包括 Ashifa Trivedi 和 Stephen Tomlin 在内的医疗保健专业人士指出了该计划的积极影响,但强调需要提高认识和公平获取,以确保更多婴儿受益。研究结果表明,尽管有些婴儿仍未受到保护,但孕产妇免疫接种可有效减少新生儿严重 RSV 疾病,这强调了继续努力增加 疫苗 吸收的重要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
孕产妇 RSV 疫苗接种 计划于 2024 年 9 月启动,其全面影响在 2025/2026 年冬季观察到。该报告于2026年8月13日发布。
地点与对象
该研究涉及英国 20 个 NHS 信托机构,重点关注 6 个月以下的婴儿。
事件
英国健康安全局 (UKHSA) 报告称,与上一年相比,2025/2026 年冬季冬季 RSV 胸部感染婴儿入住 ICU 和 HDU 的人数减少了 50%。这一减少归因于孕产妇 RSV 疫苗接种 计划。
规模、影响与风险
2025/2026 年冬季,有 63 名 6 个月以下婴儿因确诊 RSV 胸部感染而入住 ICU 或 HDU,低于上一年的 131 名。该报告还指出,2025 年 3 月至 2026 年 2 月期间,61.7% 的分娩妇女收到了 RSV 疫苗。
应对、状态与不确定性
UKHSA 表示,预计该计划要到 2025/2026 年冬季才会产生全面影响。医疗保健专业人士强调了提高认识和确保公平获得 疫苗 的重要性。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Maternal RSV vaccine halves newborn ICU admissions, says UKHSA - The Pharmaceutical Journal

News Brief
The UK Health Security Agency (UKHSA) reported that the maternal RSV vaccination programme, which began in September 2024, led to a 50% reduction in ICU and HDU admissions for infants under 6 months with RSV chest infection during the winter 2025/2026 season, compared to 131 cases the previous year. This significant decrease was observed across 20 NHS trusts participating in the surveillance. The report highlighted that 61.7% of women giving birth had received the RSV vaccine between March 2025 and February 2026. Healthcare professionals, including Ashifa Trivedi and Stephen Tomlin, noted the positive impact of the programme but emphasized the need for improved awareness and equitable access to ensure more infants benefit. The findings suggest that maternal immunisation is effective in reducing severe RSV disease in newborns, though some infants remain unprotected, underscoring the importance of continued efforts to increase vaccine uptake.
Original source text (English)

PJ Jobs Jobseekers Recruiters Subscribe Access provided by Home News Maternal RSV vaccine halves newborn ICU admissions, says UKHSA There were 63 infants admitted to intensive care or higher dependency hospital units with RSV chest infection in winter 2025/2026, down from 131 the year prior. The maternal RSV programme started in September 2024 REC Stock Footage / Shutterstock.com The number of infants admitted to intensive care with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) reduced by half after the first full year of the maternal RSV vaccination programme, according to the UK Health Security Agency (UKHSA)’s RSV annual report, published on 13 August 2026. Across the 20 NHS trusts taking part in the UKHSA surveillance, there were 63 infants under 6 months age admitted to intensive care units (ICU) or higher dependency units (HDU) with confirmed RSV chest infection in the winter season 2025/2026. The previous year, the same 20 trusts reported 131 infants aged under 6 months were admitted to ICU or HDU, the UKHSA said. “This demonstrates the strong impact that the first full year of the maternal programme has had in protecting newborns from severe RSV disease,” it added. While the maternal RSV programme started in September 2024, its full impact on reducing the number of cases and severity of RSV in infants was not expected to be seen until the winter of 2025/2026, as more pregnant women became eligible over the course of the past year, UKHSA said. Ashifa Trivedi, neonatal pharmacist and clinical lead at Evelina London Children’s Hospital, said the results were “really encouraging to see”. “For those of us working in paediatrics, we know just how unwell young babies with RSV can become, and the impact this has on babies, families and paediatric services. This data shows [sic] what effective prevention can achieve,” she said. Stephen Tomlin, clinical lead for the neonatal and paediatric pharmacy group (NPPG), said: “A 50% reduction in ICU and HDU admissions among infants under six months is a significant achievement and demonstrates the value of protecting babies before they are born. “Preventing babies from requiring intensive care is one of the clearest measures of success for any vaccination programme, and these findings suggest that maternal immunisation is already delivering substantial benefits for families and the NHS.” The report also revealed that 61.7% of women giving birth had received an RSV vaccine between March 2025 and February 2026. Trivedi commented: “There is still more we can do to ensure every eligible woman has the opportunity to make an informed choice about vaccination and that access is equitable across all communities. As healthcare professionals we have an important role in supporting conversations with families, providing clear and trusted information, and helping to address barriers to vaccination so that more babies can benefit from this protection.” Tomlin added: “While uptake exceeds 60%, thousands of infants remain unprotected each year. Every contact that healthcare professionals have with pregnant women is an opportunity to discuss the importance of RSV vaccination and its role in safeguarding newborns during their most vulnerable months of life... Improving awareness of RSV vaccination during pregnancy should remain a priority across maternity, primary care and pharmacy services.” Last updated 13 August 2026 14:03 Citation The Pharmaceutical Journal, PJ August 2026, Vol 321, No 8012;321(8012)::DOI:10.1211/PJ.2026.1.424477 Please leave a comment Cancel reply You must be logged in to post a comment. Time
The maternal RSV vaccination programme started in September 2024, and its full impact was observed in the winter season 2025/2026. The report was published on 13 August 2026.
Location and population
The study involved 20 NHS trusts in the UK, focusing on infants under 6 months of age.
Event
The UK Health Security Agency (UKHSA) reported a 50% reduction in ICU and HDU admissions for infants with RSV chest infection during the winter 2025/2026 season compared to the previous year. This reduction is attributed to the maternal RSV vaccination programme.
Scale, impact and risk
In the winter 2025/2026, 63 infants under 6 months were admitted to ICU or HDU with confirmed RSV chest infection, down from 131 in the previous year. The report also noted that 61.7% of women giving birth had received an RSV vaccine between March 2025 and February 2026.
Response, status and uncertainty
The UKHSA stated that the full impact of the programme was not expected until the winter of 2025/2026. Healthcare professionals emphasized the importance of improving awareness and ensuring equitable access to the vaccine.

RSV 孕产妇 疫苗 婴儿重症监护入院人数减半 - Medscape

RSV Maternal Vaccine Halves Baby Intensive Care Admissions - Medscape
新闻简报根据英国健康安全局 2026 年 8 月 16 日发布的数据,英国首个全年孕产妇 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疫苗接种 计划已将 6 个月以下婴儿的重症监护入院人数减少了大约一半。在 20 个参与的 NHS 信托机构中,确诊 RSV 毛细支气管炎 的 ICU 或高度依赖病房入院人数从 2024-25 年的 131 家下降到 2024 年的 25 家。2025-26 年为 63 人,而高峰紧急就诊人数较低且赛季较短。该计划面向 28 周以上的孕妇,其接受率在英格兰、威尔士和苏格兰分别为 61.7%、56.2% 和 53.7%。UKHSA 早些时候的数据显示,孕产妇单次剂量可提供超过 80% 的新生儿入院保护。官员们还报告称,在遵循常规 疫苗接种 后,75-80 岁成年人的 RSV 住院率持续下降了 40%。尽管取得了进展,当局警告说,由于覆盖不全面,数千名婴儿仍然得不到保护。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

RSV Maternal Vaccine Halves Baby Intensive Care Admissions - Medscape

News Brief
The UK’s first full year of a maternal respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccination programme has cut intensive care admissions for babies under six months by roughly half, according to figures released by the UK Health Security Agency on 16 August 2026. In the 20 participating NHS trusts, ICU or high-dependency unit admissions for confirmed RSV bronchiolitis fell from 131 in 2024–25 to 63 in 2025–26, while peak emergency attendances were lower and the season shorter. The programme, offered to pregnant women from 28 weeks, achieved uptake of 61.7% in England, 56.2% in Wales, and 53.7% in Scotland. Earlier UKHSA data showed a single maternal dose provided over 80% protection against hospital admission for newborns. Officials also reported a sustained 40% drop in RSV hospitalisations among adults aged 75–80 following routine vaccination. Despite the gains, authorities warned that thousands of infants remain unprotected due to incomplete coverage.
Original source text (English)
Medscape AI News & Perspective Tools & Reference CME/CE More EN RegisterLog In AI Mode Medscape News UK RSV Maternal Vaccine Halves Baby Intensive Care Admissions Rob Hicks, MBBS August 16, 2026 0 Add to Email Alerts Jump to Key Points The NHS maternal vaccine programme for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) has “halved” the number of babies needing support in ICUs in its first full year, new figures suggest. The UK Health Security Agency’s (UKHSA) respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) annual report showed the impact of the maternal vaccination programme for the first time. It found that around 50% fewer infants under 6 months had been admitted to intensive care with RSV disease in England. The UKHSA said that as the vaccination programme took fuller effect, there was lower RSV activity. According to the agency, last winter (2025-2026), in the 20 NHS trusts taking part in the surveillance, 63 infants younger than 6 months were admitted to ICU or higher-dependency units with confirmed RSV chest infection. The previous year, 2024-2025, the same 20 trusts reported 131 admissions. Officials said the reduction in admissions “demonstrates the strong impact that the first full year of the maternal programme has had in protecting newborns from severe RSV disease.” SUGGESTED FOR YOU Many Babies Still At Risk The maternal vaccination programme — which began in September 2024 — is offered to women from 28 weeks of pregnancy to protect newborns, who are at higher risk for severe illness from RSV. Earlier this year, a UKHSA study presented at the ESCMID conference showed a dose of the maternal vaccine provided over 80% protection to a newborn infant against hospital admission. However, the UKHSA warned that many thousands of babies were still at risk. In England, in the 12 months from March 2025 to February 2026, 61.7% of women giving birth had received an RSV vaccine. In Wales, in the 12 months from June 2025 to May 2026, this figure was 56.2%. In Scotland, 53.7% of pregnant women who reached 28 weeks of pregnancy had received an RSV vaccine since the start of the programme. Dr Conall Watson, immunisation consultant at the UKHSA, said in a press release that RSV caused a “horrible” chest infection, but is now preventable with a vaccine that he described as highly effective with an excellent safety profile. Infant Bronchiolitis Lower The UKHSA pointed out that whilst there is no risk-free birth month, babies born in late summer or the autumn are most likely to be admitted to hospital from RSV. The UK RSV season usually started in October; however, last winter cases did not start to rise until early November, peaking during late December. They then steadily declined and reached low levels in March 2026. In England in 2025-2026, peak weekly infant A&E attendances for bronchiolitis — where most cases are due to RSV — were lower than in previous seasons, and the peak season was shorter. The report also found that 65% of eligible older adults had received an RSV vaccine by the end of May this year, and that vaccination in England had resulted in a sustained reduction of over 40% in RSV-related hospitalisation rates among eligible adults 75-80 years of age. “Thanks to NHS staff dozens of new-borns have potentially avoided being admitted to intensive care after their mother received her RSV jab during pregnancy,” said Caroline Temmink, NHS director of vaccinations, in a press release. Dr Mike McKean, vice-president for policy at the Royal College of Paediatrics and Child Health, told Medscape UK News that it was “fantastic” to see newborn ICU admissions halved since the introduction of the RSV vaccine. “It shows how vital this vaccine is in protecting newborns from this nasty infection and helping to ease pressure on NHS services,” he said. Rob Hicks is a retired National Health Service doctor. A well-known TV and radio broadcaster, he has written several books and has regularly contributed to national newspapers, magazines, and online publications. He is based in the United Kingdom. B routine priorityPublic-health news

Newborn ICU admissions drop 50% in first year of RSV vaccine programme - Open Access Government

News Brief
A UK Health Security Agency report shows a 50% reduction in intensive care admissions for infants under six months with confirmed respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) during the 2025–2026 winter season, compared with the prior year. The decline follows the first full year of a maternal vaccination programme launched in September 2024, which offers the vaccine to pregnant women from 28 weeks gestation. Surveillance across 20 NHS trusts recorded 63 such admissions in 2025–2026, down from 131 in 2024–2025. The programme also coincided with lower emergency department attendances for infant bronchiolitis and an earlier end to the RSV season. Among older adults, hospitalisations for RSV fell by over 40% in those aged 75–80 years by May 2026, with 65% of eligible adults vaccinated. Health officials urge pregnant women to get vaccinated before the autumn virus season.
Original source text (English)

COVID-19 Research Technology Artificial Intelligence Digital Transformation Transport North America Asia Search Home Open Access News Health & Social Care Newborn ICU admissions drop 50% in first year of RSV vaccine programme Open Access News Health & Social Care Newborn ICU admissions drop 50% in first year of RSV vaccine programme August 13, 2026 image: ©Hailshadow| iStock Data from the UK Health Security Agency shows that admissions of infants under six months to intensive care for respiratory syncytial virus dropped by 50 per cent during the first full year of the maternal vaccination programme According to the UKHSA annual report, surveillance across 20 NHS trusts recorded 63 infants under six months admitted to intensive care or high dependency units with confirmed RSV chest infections during the 2025–2026 winter season. This represents a 50 per cent decrease from the 131 admissions reported by the same hospital trusts in 2024–2025. The report evaluates the initial impact of the maternal vaccination scheme introduced in September 2024, which offers the vaccine to pregnant women from 28 weeks of pregnancy. Between March 2025 and February 2026, 61.7 per cent of women giving birth had received the vaccine. Maternal antibodies pass through the placenta to provide newborns with immediate protection against severe disease from birth. Reduced A&E demand and altered seasonal trends In addition to reducing intensive care admissions, the maternal vaccine contributed to lower hospital emergency department attendances for bronchiolitis, a common lung infection primarily caused by RSV. Peak weekly A&E attendances for infant bronchiolitis were lower than in previous years, and the peak season was shorter. The overall 2025–2026 RSV season started later than usual, with cases rising in early November, peaking in December, and declining to low levels by March 2026. Prior UKHSA research presented at the ESCMID conference indicates that maternal vaccination provides over 80 per cent protection against hospital admission for newborn infants. Senior adult outcomes and broader impact The annual report also examined the impact of the RSV vaccine rollout among older adults. By the end of May 2026, 65 per cent of eligible adults had received their vaccine. Vaccine impact analysis across England demonstrated a sustained reduction of over 40 per cent in RSV-related hospitalisations among adults aged 75 to 80 years. Health officials advise that pregnant women past 28 weeks contact their maternity services or GP practices to arrange vaccination prior to the autumn virus season. SK bioscience takes Gates-backed RSV antibody into infant trial in South Africa - Korea Biomedical Review

News Brief
SK bioscience is conducting an infant trial for an RSV antibody supported by the Gates Foundation, as reported by Korea Biomedical Review. The trial is part of broader efforts to address RSV-related health challenges, particularly in vulnerable populations. While the evidence does not specify the number of cases, deaths, or exposures, it highlights the company's focus on developing treatments for infants. The RSV vaccine Abrysvo, developed by Pfizer, has already received approval in Korea for maternal and adult prevention, indicating progress in RSV vaccine development. The trial's status is ongoing, and no further details on its outcomes or scale are provided in the supplied evidence. The information is based on a news report published on August 14, 2026, with updates provided up to August 18, 2026.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

ArkBio 的 RSV 抗病毒药物 药物 Ziresovir 获得 FDA 批准进行 II 期临床试验

ArkBio's RSV Antiviral Drug Ziresovir Receives FDA Approval for Phase II Clinical Trial
新闻简报ArkBio's RSV antiviral drug Ziresovir (AK0529) has received FDA approval for a phase II clinical trial targeting severely ill RSV-infected infants, including those in pediatric intensive care units (PICUs). The clinical study, conducted in China on patients aged 1-24 months, showed that Ziresovir significantly reduces viral load, accelerates symptom resolution, and lowers the risk of recurrent wheezing and asthma over a 24-month follow-up period. RSV is a major cause of lower respiratory tract infections (LRTIs) in infants, leading to 33 million cases, 3.6 million hospitalizations, and over 100,000 deaths annually. Severely ill RSV-infected infants face rapid disease progression, high risk, and significant medical burden. The phase II trial aims to evaluate Ziresovir's efficacy, particularly in reducing oxygen treatment duration and improving recovery time, providing a clinical basis for future registrational trials. The approval marks a key milestone in the global development of Ziresovir for RSV treatment.
查看新闻五要素
时间
美国 FDA 批准开展 II 期临床试验的公告发布于 2026-08-14。针对 1-24 月龄患者的临床研究在中国开展,研究结果于 2025 年发表。
地点与对象
该临床试验的重点是需要在 PICU(主要是在美国)住院的严重 RSV 感染婴儿。
事件
ArkBio 的 RSV 抗病毒药物 药物 Ziresovir (AK0529) 获得 FDA 批准,用于对严重 RSV 感染婴儿患者(包括 PICU 中的婴儿)进行 II 期临床试验。临床研究表明,齐瑞索韦可以显着降低病毒载量,加速呼吸道症状的缓解,并降低复发性喘息和哮喘的风险。
规模、影响与风险
RSV is the leading pathogen of lower respiratory tract infections (LRTIs) in infants and young children globally, causing approximately 33 million LRTI cases, 3.6 million hospitalizations, and more than 100,000 deaths annually. Severely ill RSV infected infants often require PICU admission, with rapid disease progression, high risk, and a heavy medical burden.
应对、状态与不确定性
FDA批准了II期临床试验,旨在评估Ziresovir的临床疗效,特别是吸氧时长治疗和持续恢复时间。临床研究结果已发表在知名期刊上,该试验是 Ziresovir 全球临床开发的一部分。

ArkBio's RSV Antiviral Drug Ziresovir Receives FDA Approval for Phase II Clinical Trial

News Brief
ArkBio's RSV antiviral drug Ziresovir (AK0529) has received FDA approval for a phase II clinical trial targeting severely ill RSV-infected infants, including those in pediatric intensive care units (PICUs). The clinical study, conducted in China on patients aged 1-24 months, showed that Ziresovir significantly reduces viral load, accelerates symptom resolution, and lowers the risk of recurrent wheezing and asthma over a 24-month follow-up period. RSV is a major cause of lower respiratory tract infections (LRTIs) in infants, leading to 33 million cases, 3.6 million hospitalizations, and over 100,000 deaths annually. Severely ill RSV-infected infants face rapid disease progression, high risk, and significant medical burden. The phase II trial aims to evaluate Ziresovir's efficacy, particularly in reducing oxygen treatment duration and improving recovery time, providing a clinical basis for future registrational trials. The approval marks a key milestone in the global development of Ziresovir for RSV treatment.
Original source text (English)
SHANGHAI , Aug. 14, 2026 /PRNewswire/ -- Shanghai Ark Biopharmaceutical Co., Ltd. ("ArkBio") today announced that the U.S. Food and Drug Administration (FDA) has approved ziresovir (AK0529) for a phase II clinical trial in the severely ill RSV infected infant patients, including the ones in pediatric intensive care unit (PICU). Severe RSV Infection: An Urgent Unmet Need in PICU RSV is the leading pathogen of lower respiratory tract infections (LRTIs) in infants and young children globally, causing approximately 33 million LRTI cases, 3.6 million hospitalizations, and more than 100,000 deaths annually. Severely ill RSV infected infants often require admission to the PICU for intensive care such as respiratory support, with rapid disease progression, high risk and a heavy medical burden. There is currently no approved RSV-specific antiviral therapy worldwide; clinical management remains limited to symptomatic and supportive care, such as oxygenation, leaving an urgent unmet need for effective antiviral treatment in hospitalized RSV infected patients with severe diseases. Ziresovir Advances to Treat Severely Infected Infant Patients Ziresovir is the first direct RSV antiviral drug to successfully complete a phase III clinical trial with positive results. The clinical study in 1-24 months age patients conducted in China demonstrated that ziresovir can significantly reduce viral load, accelerate the resolution of respiratory symptoms, and significantly lower the risk of recurrent wheezing and asthma over 24-month follow-up period, with a favorable safety profile. Key findings from the clinical study have been published in The New England Journal of Medicine and The Lancet Child & Adolescent Health . In 2025, ziresovir was included as the only therapeutic drug in the WHO's Paediatric Drug Optimization for Respiratory Syncytial Virus publication. The newly approved phase II clinical trial marks the extension of ziresovir's clinical development into the most critically ill RSV patients, representing a key milestone in the clinical development of ziresovir globally. Get the latest news delivered to your inbox Sign up for The Manila Times newsletters By signing up with an email address, I acknowledge that I have read and agree to the Terms of Service and Privacy Policy . FDA Approved Phase II Clinical Trial The approved multicenter, randomized, double-blind, placebo-controlled phase II clinical trial will focus on severely ill RSV infected infants requiring hospitalization in PICU, one of the most critical RSV patient populations with the most urgent clinical treatment needs. This clinical trial is designed to evaluate clinical efficacy of ziresovir, especially the length of oxygen treatment and sustained recovery time, providing the clinical basis for the future registrational trials. About ArkBio ArkBio is a commercial-stage biotechnology company focused on the discovery and development of innovative therapeutics for respiratory/lung and pediatric diseases. Founded in 2014, the company has established proprietary technology platforms and a differentiated R&D pipeline through internal innovation and strategic collaborations. Key pipeline assets include: ziresovir (AK0529), the first direct-acting antiviral for RSV with positive phase 3 results; AK3280, a potentially best-in-class anti-fibrotic agent with positive phase 2 results in idiopathic pulmonary fibrosis, currently in phase 3 registrational trials; AK0901, approved and commercialized in China for ADHD treatment. The company also has multiple first-in-class or best-in-class innovative drug candidates in clinical or preclinical development. ArkBio has established strategic partnerships with multinational pharmaceutical companies including Roche and Genentech, leading academic institutions including The Scripps Research Institute and the Institute of Microbiology of the Chinese Academy of Sciences, Qilu Pharmaceutical, as well as other domestic and international biotech companies and CROs. For more information, please visit: www.arkbiosciences.com Investor Inquiries: [email protected]

The clinical study demonstrated that Ziresovir can significantly reduce viral load, accelerate the resolution of respiratory symptoms, and lower the risk of recurrent wheezing and asthma.
Scale, impact and risk

Severely ill RSV infected infants often require PICU admission, with rapid disease progression, high risk, and a heavy medical burden.
Response, status and uncertainty
The FDA approved the phase II clinical trial, which is designed to evaluate the clinical efficacy of Ziresovir, especially the length of oxygen treatment and sustained recovery time. The clinical study results have been published in reputable journals, and the trial is part of the global clinical development of Ziresovir.
